[1] Rethinking in Spikes: Mitigating Hallucinations in MDLMs with Step-Aware Decoding cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Zhongxing Xu, Zhonghua Wang, Zhe Qian, Shiyan Su, Ming Hu

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态扩散语言模型（MDLMs）中的幻觉问题，发现迭代去噪过程中少数步骤会出现与幻觉相关的局部熵尖峰。为此提出即插即用的解码策略 StepRefine，通过步骤感知地在离散去噪与潜在细化之间切换，在熵尖峰步骤保留概率加权嵌入并进行多轮连续细化，以缓解幻觉。

Details

Motivation: 现有MDLMs通过迭代去噪进行全局建模，但离散 token 提交会丢弃掩码位置的概率分布信息，导致熵尖峰步骤中候选语义与上下文交互不足，从而引发幻觉；论文希望识别并修正这些异常去噪步骤。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值，但宣称在多个多模态基准上、针对不同MDLMs均能显著缓解幻觉，说明方法具有跨模型和跨基准的通用有效性。

Insight: 创新点在于从去噪步熵偏差角度识别幻觉相关异常步骤，并提出步骤感知的离散去噪与潜在细化切换机制；客观看，其可借鉴之处是将解码过程中的不确定性动态监控与概率加权嵌入细化结合，形成即插即用的幻觉缓解方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal diffusion language models (MDLMs) have exhibited strong global modeling capabilities through iterative refinement. We observe that mask uncertainty follows an overall downward trend during iterative denoising, while a few denoising steps exhibit localized entropy spikes closely associated with hallucinations. We argue that anomalous steps can be identified by measuring the entropy deviation of each denoising step from its neighboring temporal window. We hypothesize that discrete token commitment discards the distributional information at masked positions, limiting the continued interaction between candidate semantics and the context during entropy spikes. With this goal, we present StepRefine, a plug-and-play decoding strategy that leverages semantic context to achieve reliable denoising. The core of our method lies in a step-aware switch between discrete denoising and latent refinement. The model employs probability-weighted embeddings at entropy spike steps to preserve diverse reasoning hypotheses and performs multiple rounds of continuous refinement. Meanwhile, a refinement controller monitors distributional stability to reduce unnecessary loops and switch the model back to standard discrete denoising. Through extensive experiments, StepRefine demonstrates significant hallucination mitigation across different MDLMs on multimodal benchmarks.

[2] TimeOmni-o1: Bidirectionally Coupling Time Series Foundation Models and LLMs for Multimodal Reasoning and Forecasting cs.CLPDF

Ziyang Zhang, Shenyi Li, Wen Wu, Chao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 TimeOmni-o1，将预训练时间序列基础模型（TSFM）与大语言模型（LLM）双向耦合，以同时支持时间序列理解问答和未来值预测。模型一方面用 TSFM 将长序列压缩编码后交给 LLM 联合文本推理，另一方面将 LLM 的推理结果反馈给 TSFM 作为预测条件。作者还构建了带推理轨迹的多模态时间序列推理数据集 MMTR，覆盖 12 个领域、50 万以上样本，并在理解和预测任务上验证了效果。

Details

Motivation: 现实时间序列常伴随文本，理解和预测都需要序列与文本联合推理；但 LLM 将序列当作数字 token，难以处理长序列，TSFM 虽预测强却不能读写文本，现有时间序列语言模型又未充分利用 TSFM 的预训练预测能力。

Result: 在 MMTR 数据集上，TimeOmni-o1 在大多数领域的理解和预测任务上优于现有模型，也优于单独使用的内部 LLM 和 TSFM；在三个开放基准上，其表现优于专用时间序列模型。

Insight: 创新点在于双向耦合 TSFM 与 LLM：TSFM 为 LLM 提供压缩序列表示，LLM 的文本推理又反过来条件化 TSFM 的预测，从而统一读写序列和文本并发挥二者预训练优势；同时构建了带推理轨迹的大规模多模态时序推理数据集。

Abstract: Time series from real-world systems are often accompanied by text, such as a report on an event that affects them. Two common tasks on these series are understanding, e.g., answering questions about a series, and forecasting its future values. Both tasks require reasoning over the series and the text together. Moreover, understanding outputs text while forecasting outputs a time series, so serving both requires a model that reads and generates both modalities. Large language models (LLMs) read time series as digit tokens and handle long series poorly. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast well from large-scale pretraining but typically neither read nor generate text. Time series language models add a time series modality to the LLM but forecast on the LLM side, leaving the TSFM’s pretrained forecasting ability unused. We present TimeOmni-o1, which couples a pretrained TSFM and an LLM bidirectionally. In one direction, the TSFM encodes the series in compressed form for the LLM, which reasons over it together with the text. In the other, the LLM’s reasoning is fed back into the TSFM, which produces the forecast conditioned on it. One model architecture thus reads and generates both series and text, reasons in text, and draws on the TSFM’s pretrained forecasting ability. To train it, we build MMTR, a multimodal time series reasoning dataset with annotated reasoning traces, covering both tasks across 12 domains with 500k+ samples. On MMTR, TimeOmni-o1 outperforms existing models on both understanding and forecasting across most domains, as well as the LLM and the TSFM inside it when used individually. The gains carry over to three open benchmarks, where it outperforms the dedicated time series models.

[3] Actionable Feedback: Improving Model Capabilities from User Edit Data on Clinical Documentation cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Daniel P. Jeong, Yewon Byun, John Giorgi, Peng Xu, Zachary C. Lipton

TL;DR: 论文关注临床文档场景中LLM输出经临床医生编辑后能否用于模型训练的问题。作者发现真实部署数据中超过45%的编辑包含输入不支持的附加信息，直接微调会诱导幻觉；为此提出临床医生编辑行为分类法，基于MIMIC-III构建半合成编辑数据，并用简单过滤方法筛选可操作编辑。实验表明，逐步过滤至有依据的编辑可在保持输入忠实性的同时提升临床医生对齐指标。

Details

Motivation: 临床医生对LLM文档输出的编辑本可作为可扩展监督信号，但真实编辑中大量内容并非由模型输入支持，朴素微调可能教模型产生无依据内容。论文旨在识别并利用真正可操作的编辑反馈，避免从噪声编辑中学习幻觉。

Result: 在基于MIMIC-III出院摘要生成的半合成数据集上，朴素微调原始编辑输出会增加无依据内容；采用逐步过滤至有依据编辑的方法可提升临床医生对齐指标，同时保持对输入上下文的忠实性。摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA比较。

Insight: 创新点在于结合真实部署产品数据构建临床医生编辑行为分类法，并据此生成半合成编辑数据，再通过简单过滤识别可操作编辑。客观看，其价值在于将用户编辑视为带噪监督信号，强调筛选grounded edits以兼顾利用编辑反馈和抑制幻觉。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in clinical documentation workflows, where clinicians review and edit their outputs before use. In principle, these clinician edits represent a scalable source of supervision for model training. In practice, not all edits represent actionable feedback: for instance, on data drawn from real-world deployment of an AI clinical documentation product, our review suggests that over 45% of edited outputs contain user-added information that is not directly supported by the inputs to the model. Naively fine-tuning on such edits risks teaching the model to hallucinate. In this paper, we develop a method to address this challenge. Given that real clinician edits on deployed documentation tools are not publicly available, we first develop a taxonomy of clinician-editing behaviors, informed by data from a deployed AI clinical documentation product. Then, we develop a method for generating semi-synthetic clinician-edit data, informed by our taxonomy, on a discharge-summary generation task based on MIMIC-III, a public dataset. Finally, we introduce simple filtering methods for identifying actionable edits, with accuracy evaluated by expert review. We show empirically, on our semi-synthetic dataset, that naive fine-tuning on raw edited output increases ungrounded content, while our approach of progressively filtering toward grounded edits enables improvement on clinician-alignment metrics while retaining faithfulness to the input context.

[4] Catch It Where It Happens: Real Time Error Correction During LLM Reasoning cs.CLPDF

Rotem Elimelech, Michael Shalyt, Ido Kaminer

TL;DR: 论文提出一种在LLM推理过程中实时进行确定性验证与纠错的方法：当模型逐步生成推理时，用代码/计算机代数系统即时检查可验证的步骤，发现错误就指出并让模型从当前位置继续修正，避免错误传播和从头重试。作者在符号数学任务上验证该方法，使用ASyMoB基准和三个开源权重模型，证明无需额外验证LLM或GPU调用即可提升准确率并减少错误答案。

Details

Motivation: LLM在数学、科学和工程推理中常出现自信但难发现的错误，单步错误会传播并浪费推理token。现有方法要么只检查最终答案并重新开始，要么依赖验证LLM，带来GPU竞争和响应变慢。

Result: 在ASyMoB基准上，使用三个开源权重模型，在线验证将准确率提升1.4-5.6个百分点，相当于错误答案净减少1.8-14.9%，同时token使用量仅增加6-7%。

Insight: 创新点在于将验证从最终答案后置检查转为推理过程中的实时、确定性、无模型调用检查，并在错误处原地纠偏。客观看，其优势是模型无关、低开销且可与LLM进步互补；但当前主要适用于可由计算机代数系统等程序检查的符号数学步骤，扩展到更多推理类型依赖确定性检查器的覆盖范围。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted with mathematical, scientific and engineering work, yet their mistakes are often confidently written and hard to spot. A single wrong step tends to propagate, making the model waste inference tokens following a false premise. Existing safeguards either check only the final answer and restart from scratch, or rely on a verifier LLM that competes for GPU capacity and slows down response time. We propose online, deterministic verification: many kinds of reasoning steps can be validated by code, so a step can be verified in real time, as it is written, without any model call or GPU use. Whenever a check fails, we point out the mistake and let the model continue from the same spot. We demonstrate this approach on symbolic mathematics, where derivations already contain explicit expressions that a computer algebra system can check in milliseconds. Tested on the ASyMoB benchmark with three open-weights models, our online validation improves accuracy by 1.4-5.6 percentage points, corresponding to a 1.8-14.9% net reduction in incorrect answers, while increasing token usage by only 6-7%. Because the verifier is independent of the model, the approach complements progress in LLMs rather than competing with it: every new model can benefit immediately, and the benefit grows as deterministic checkers extend to more kinds of reasoning steps.

[5] Deletion Sensitivity Is a Property of the Model: A Three-Condition Measurement across Seven Open Reasoning Models cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.SEPDF

Hanzhang Jia, Liheng Zeng, Zhiyu Yang, Yi Gao, Bo Ma

TL;DR: 论文研究了多轮工具调用中推理模型因跨发行方转发导致推理历史被删除（reasoning-history deletion）所带来的任务性能损失，首次进行了直接、盲测、多随机种子的测量：在三种渲染条件、七个开源推理模型、三个随机种子、794个任务和官方检查器下评估。结果显示同一删除操作造成的性能变化从-4.4到+51.3分不等，且删除敏感度是模型层面的固有属性，在不同部署间保持稳定，并提出用两格筛选（一次保留运行+一次删除运行）低成本重测模型。

Details

Motivation: 推理模型在多轮工具会话中每轮都会写入并读取推理轨迹，而73%的用户交互跨越多个轮次；当请求跨上游发行方转发时，其历史会被删除，但这一删除带来的任务性能代价此前未被测量。

Result: 在794个任务、七个开源推理模型、三个种子和官方检查器上，同一删除带来的性能变化范围为-4.4到+51.3分且两端均不含零；对某一模型将删除比例从0调至100%，读数变化不超过1.5分，表明该敏感度读数是模型层面的稳定属性。

Insight: 创新点在于首次对多轮工具调用中的推理历史删除进行直接、盲测、多随机种子的系统测量，并提出’删除敏感度是模型属性’这一可测量结论、其在部署间保持稳定、以及可用两格筛选低成本重测；客观来看，把这一读数作为路由与聚合的先验信息而非隐形默认成本，对多发行方部署具有实际指导价值。

Abstract: Reasoning models write a reasoning trace at every turn of a multi-turn tool session, and later turns read this history. Across 94k ShareGPT conversations, 73 percent of user interactions span multiple turns. Upstream providers bind each trace to its issuer, so a request forwarded across issuers arrives with its history deleted, and the task cost of this stripping remains unmeasured. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first direct, blind, multi-seed measurement of full reasoning-history deletion in multi-turn tool calling, through three rendering conditions, seven open-weight reasoning models, three seeds, 794 tasks, and the official checker. The same deletion spans -4.4 to +51.3 points with both ends excluding zero, and for the one model re-dosed from 0 to 100 percent deletion, the reading varies by at most 1.5 points. Deletion sensitivity is a model-level property. The reading follows the model itself and stays constant across deployments, and a retrained or re-released model is a new model that a two-cell screen, one retention run and one deletion run, re-reads for the cost of two runs. Deletion sensitivity is thus a measurable property of the model, and the reading must enter routing and aggregation as prior information rather than persist as an invisible default cost.

[6] What drives the emergence of symbolic mechanisms in large language models? cs.CLPDF

Melody Zixuan Li, Taylor Webb

TL;DR: 论文探究大语言模型中符号机制涌现的驱动因素。作者在抽象序列任务上从头训练不同架构和数据分布的网络，并通过表征、注意力和因果中介分析发现，数据多样性越高符号特征越强，且该现象在修改的Transformer乃至MLP中也出现，说明架构归纳偏置并非必要条件。

Details

Motivation: 已有研究发现LLM具备支持抽象推理的涌现符号机制，但尚不清楚哪些因素驱动其出现，论文旨在区分架构与数据分布的作用。

Result: 实验确认从头训练的Transformer可发展出符号机制；在多个数据多样性维度上，多样性越高符号签名越强；修改架构甚至MLP也出现类似签名，实体-属性绑定任务结果一致，表明数据多样性而非架构是主要驱动。

Insight: 论文通过受控训练和多种机制分析系统分离架构与数据因素，提出数据多样性是符号计算涌现的主要驱动力，挑战了架构归纳偏置必要性的观点；可借鉴之处在于用抽象任务从头训练研究涌现机制，并结合因果中介分析验证。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a set of emergent symbolic mechanisms that support abstract reasoning in large language models, but it remains unclear what factors drive the emergence of these mechanisms. To address this question, we trained neural networks from scratch on abstract sequence tasks, varying both architectural and data distributional factors, and investigated the effects of these factors on the emergence of symbolic mechanisms. Using representational, attentional, and causal mediation analyses, we first confirmed that transformer-based language models trained from scratch on our task developed symbolic mechanisms. We then found that, across multiple axes of data diversity, greater diversity produced stronger symbolic signatures. Surprisingly, the same signatures emerged in modified transformer architectures and even multilayer perceptrons, indicating that architectural inductive biases are not necessary for the development of symbolic mechanisms. We also observed consistent patterns in an entity-attribute binding task. Together, these findings identify data diversity, not architecture, as the primary driver of emergent symbolic computation in neural networks.

[7] BioMem: Agentic Memory for Biomedical LLM Agents cs.CLPDF

Mingchen Li, Yongkang Xiao, Rumeng Li, Rui Zhang, Hong Yu

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了生物医学领域中LLM智能体的记忆机制，评估了五种记忆方法在四个LLM上的表现，涵盖知识推理、临床预测和长期对话记忆三个场景，并提出了无训练的混合记忆检索与利用框架HMRU，在多个基准上提升了性能。

Details

Motivation: 生物医学LLM智能体需要有效可靠的记忆系统来保留、检索、更新和利用跨任务的信息，但现有记忆系统在生物医学领域的有效性尚不明确，论文旨在系统评估并改进。

Result: HMRU在生物医学问答和对话记忆基准上平均相对提升8.6%，对话回答错误减少24.9%，缩小与全上下文推理性能差距的48.0%，并在部分临床预测任务上也有提升。

Insight: 论文系统分析了记忆构建、证据相关性和对话历史对下游性能的影响，创新性地提出了结合证据可访问性和任务感知利用的无训练框架，为开发更有效的记忆增强LLM系统提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable potential in biomedical applications, while agentic memory systems have emerged as a promising approach to preserving and utilizing information across tasks and interactions. However, a fundamental question remains: how effectively and reliably do these systems retain, retrieve, update, and utilize biomedical information? In this study, we systematically investigate agentic memory in the biomedical domain, focusing on task effectiveness, retrieval quality, information fidelity, and long-term robustness. We evaluate five memory approaches across four LLMs in three representative settings: biomedical knowledge reasoning, clinical prediction, and long-term conversational memory. Our analyses reveal how memory construction, evidence relevance, and conversational history shape downstream biomedical performance, highlighting the distinct challenges of retrieving relevant information, preserving its fidelity, and effectively utilizing it for reasoning. Building on these findings, we introduce Hybrid Memory Retrieval and Utilization (HMRU), a training-free framework that jointly enhances evidence accessibility and task-aware utilization to improve biomedical reasoning and long-term conversational memory. HMRU achieves an average relative improvement of 8.6% across biomedical question-answering and conversational memory benchmarks, with additional gains in selected clinical prediction tasks. It further reduces conversational answer errors by 24.9% and closes 48.0% of the performance gap to full-context inference. Our findings provide a systematic characterization of biomedical agentic memory and practical insights into developing more effective and reliable memory-augmented LLM systems.

[8] How Post-Training Influences Verbalized Algorithmic Computation in Language Models cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Niall Dalton

TL;DR: 论文研究后训练（SFT、DPO、RLVR）如何改变语言模型在多位数加法中的内部信息利用与显式思维链（CoT）作用。作者以进位值作为已知中间状态，分析Olmo-3-7B-Instruct不同后训练检查点，发现内部激活中的进位信息可被探针解码且SFT与RLVR准确率相近，但操纵该信息不能可靠控制输出；相比之下，RLVR生成的CoT在不同接收检查点上更有助于提高准确率。

Details

Motivation: 后训练能提升语言模型推理，但其如何改变模型对内部信息的利用以及显式CoT的实际作用仍不清楚。论文希望厘清SFT、DPO、RLVR等后训练阶段对算法计算中中间状态使用和书面推理有效性的影响。

Result: 在多位数加法任务上，探针从SFT和RLVR检查点内部激活中恢复进位信息的准确率相近，但干预进位信息不能稳定控制模型输出。RLVR产生的CoT在接收检查点上准确率高于SFT或DPO产生的CoT；RLVR后与进位相关的陈述获得更高重要性分数，DPO也表现出类似敏感性但没有相应准确率提升。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于用已知进位中间状态系统比较不同后训练检查点，并区分内部可解码信息、对推理陈述的敏感性与显式CoT的实际有用性。其客观启示是：后训练可能主要改变书面推理的可迁移有用性和模型对推理内容的敏感度，而更高敏感性并不必然带来更好任务表现。

Abstract: Post-training improves language-model reasoning, but how it changes the use of internal information and verbalized chain of thought (CoT) remains unclear. We study successive SFT, DPO, and RLVR checkpoints of Olmo-3-7B-Instruct on multi-digit addition, using carry values as known intermediate state. Probes recover carry information from internal activations with similar accuracy in SFT and RLVR, although manipulating this information does not reliably control model outputs. In contrast, the usefulness of verbalized reasoning differs: CoTs produced by RLVR yield higher accuracy across receiving checkpoints than those produced by SFT or DPO. We also show that carry-related statements receive higher importance scores after RLVR than matched random controls. DPO shows similar sensitivity to carry-related statements but without comparable accuracy gains. These results suggest that post-training changes the usefulness of written reasoning and sensitivity to its content in meaningful ways, although greater sensitivity does not necessarily coincide with better problem performance.

[9] An evidence-traceable benchmark of clinical reasoning in large language models cs.CLPDF

Zhiqun Zhao, Xiaodong Yang, Bruce Gu, Ming Li, Yi Fu

TL;DR: 论文构建了可追溯临床证据的医学LLM基准Med Evidence Bench（MEB），包含1536道由医生构建的题目，覆盖指南知识、标准化临床场景和真实世界实验室场景三个层级，并将每条评分标准链接到带版本的源文本片段。研究用证据追溯分数（ETS）评估十个LLM，发现模型在指南知识上表现很高，但在标准化和真实世界场景中证据追溯能力显著下降，且不同层级排名会变化。

Details

Motivation: 现有医学LLM基准的评分标准难以追溯到临床证据，导致基准分数不易解释。论文希望建立可证据追溯的临床推理评测，区分模型是仅会复述指南知识，还是能在患者特异性场景中正确使用证据。

Result: 十个LLM的ETS在指南知识为0.975-0.997，标准化场景为0.292-0.658，真实世界场景为0.182-0.447，且排名随层级变化；真实世界场景中识别临床信息均分0.770，明显高于匹配证据0.165和形成建议0.160。自动评分在1296条标准级判断上与三位医生多数投票的macro-F1为0.930（95%CI 0.915-0.945），但证据应用方面一致性较低。

Insight: 创新点在于将每条医学主张的评分标准链接到版本化源文本片段，并设计三层级场景与ETS来评估证据可追溯性。客观来看，该工作把“指南回忆”和“患者特异性证据使用”拆开评测，揭示高分指南知识并不保证真实场景中的证据匹配与建议形成；但材料为中文且未证明临床有效性。

Abstract: Benchmark scores for medical large language models (LLMs) are difficult to interpret when scoring criteria cannot be traced to clinical evidence. Here we developed Med Evidence Bench (MEB; https://www.medevidencebench.org.cn/), comprising 1,536 physician-constructed items across three levels: guideline knowledge, standardized clinical scenarios and real-world laboratory scenarios. Each criterion stating a medical claim is linked to a versioned source-text span. Across ten LLMs, the Evidence Trace Score (ETS) was 0.975-0.997 for guideline knowledge, 0.292-0.658 for standardized scenarios and 0.182-0.447 for real-world scenarios, and rankings varied across levels. For real-world scenarios, scores for identifying clinical information (mean, 0.770) exceeded those for matching evidence (0.165) or formulating recommendations (0.160). On 1,296 criterion-level judgments, automated grading agreed with the majority vote of three physicians (macro-F1, 0.930; 95% confidence interval, 0.915-0.945), although agreement was lower for evidence application. These findings from Chinese-language materials indicate that guideline recall does not ensure patient-specific evidence use and do not establish clinical effectiveness.

Details

Abstract: Objective: Concept-based interpretation has emerged as a promising approach for explaining blackbox models using human-understandable, high-level concepts. However, most existing methods require annotated concept labels to train the model to identify concepts in the input, and obtaining such annotations in clinical text is often expensive. This study aims to enable concept-based interpretation for language models without concept supervision on clinical prediction tasks. Methods: We propose a novel framework that enables concept-based interpretation without concept supervision through a tailored cross-attention bottleneck. Clinical text and the text descriptions of medical concepts are first tokenized and embedded by a pretrained language model. A cross-attention module then estimates the presence of each concept for each text token, producing concept relevance scores that capture how concepts are involved throughout the note. These scores are pooled and used in a linear prediction layer to model clinical outcomes in an interpretable manner. Results: The performance of the proposed framework was compared against blackbox models across two tasks: chest trauma severity stratification using a private dataset and an in-hospital mortality prediction task using the publicly available MIMIC-III dataset. The proposed framework achieved AUROC values within 0.02 of the corresponding blackbox models. The framework also enabled cohort- and patient-level interpretation, capturing the overall concept importance across the cohort and patient-specific concept contributions. Conclusion: Our proposed framework realizes concept-based interpretation without requiring ground-truth concept presence labels. It improves the transparency of clinical text predictions while maintaining strong performance in clinical prediction tasks.

Details

Motivation: 现有系统常把LLM与确定性事实层结合，但事实层应供给静态属性还是计算结果缺乏度量；论文关注模型在需要组合多个属性的规则推理中失败的问题。

Result: 11个开放权重模型直接回答时，干支日正确率均不超4%，碰撞项不超1%；静态属性无稳定增益，完整关系列表也难被直接利用。查询过滤事实层（探索性）将Qwen3-8B/32B提升至78–92%，另四个模型达96–100%；三个闭源推理模型单独计算碰撞达68–95%。国际象棋局面实验也显示属性不能替代关系。

Abstract: Language-model systems in rule-governed domains often pair the model with a deterministic fact layer, but which facts that layer should supply is rarely measured. We measure it in the Chinese sexagenary system underlying BaZi chart analysis, where every fact has one table-checkable value: 682 items over twelve fact categories and 160 transit–natal collision items, posed closed-book, with the reference table, and with computed facts at increasing levels. In eleven open-weight models answering directly, errors concentrate where attributes must be combined: none gets more than 4% of sexagenary days or 1% of collisions right. Static attributes have no consistent effect, and the complete relation list leaves most direct-answering models near zero although it contains the answer. A query-filtered layer (exploratory) raises Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B to 78–92% and four further models to 96–100%. Three proprietary reasoning models compute 68–95% of collisions unaided. On chess positions, under predictions locked in advance, attributes again do not substitute for relations and listed distractors are misattributed. The fact layer must supply computed interactions; how they are presented depends on the model and the domain.

[12] One Byte, One Token: Byteifying a 27B Hybrid-Attention LLM with Self-Generated Data cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Liwei Zhang, ZhiMing Tong

TL;DR: 论文研究能否将一个已经训练好的 27B 子词级 LLM（Qwen3.8-27B，48 层 Gated DeltaNet + 16 层全注意力）直接转换为纯字节级模型（一个字节一个 token），而不需要补丁结构或局部编码器。方法将词表从 248,320 压缩到 384 个条目，其余权重不变，并用原始模型 on-policy 自生成的监督数据（含百科知识文本）进行两阶段课程训练（先编码、后阅读）。结果表明模型保留了大部分能力，在字符级任务上显著提升，但牺牲了吞吐量。

Details

Motivation: 字节级 BPE 会切碎中文文本（平均 3.84 字节/token，64% token 为单字符），并隐藏拼写信息；作者希望无需重构结构、无需局部编码器，就能把已有的子词模型直接迁移为纯字节模型（一字节一 token），并尽可能保留其原有能力。

Result: 在 MATH-500、HumanEval+、MMLU-Pro 及非思考模式 GSM8K 上与原模型的差距落在配对 bootstrap 区间内，但在 C-Eval、思考模式 GSM8K 和非思考 IFEval 上下降 4-6 分；字符级任务全面领先（CUTE 75.2→84.0，EXECUTE-zho 81.6→93.9，中文汉字套件 54.4→64.0），但汉字读音、笔画等查表知识下降且思考模式无法恢复；吞吐代价明显，每步速度相同但每步产出少 3-5 倍，每秒汉字数约为原来的 1/4，64K 字节预填慢 6.5 倍。

Insight: 创新点在于以最小改动（仅压缩词表、冻结其余权重）实现子词模型到纯字节模型的原生转换，并采用原始模型 on-policy 自生成监督数据、编码优先再阅读的两阶段课程；还系统记录并修复了贪心失控、采样破坏 UTF-8、小词表惩罚项失效、重复四种字节模型失败模式，为字节级 LLM 的改造提供了可复用经验。

Abstract: Byte-level BPE fragments Chinese text (for Qwen3.8 on Chinese Wikipedia, 3.84 bytes per token on average; 64% of tokens are single characters) and hides spelling from the model. We ask whether an already-trained 27B subword model can be converted directly into a pure byte-level model (one byte, one token), without a patching structure or a local encoder, while keeping most of its ability. Starting from Qwen3.8-27B (48 Gated DeltaNet layers + 16 full-attention layers), we shrink the vocabulary from 248,320 to 384 entries (the 256 byte symbols already in the BPE vocabulary plus special ids), leave every other weight untouched, and continue training in a two-stage curriculum (encoding first, then reading) on 4.77 GB of supervised bytes: all dialogue/behavioral supervision is generated on-policy by the original model itself (character dictionary, word dictionary, general dialogue, coding sessions), and knowledge reading uses encyclopedia text (54% of supervised bytes). Training takes 178.6 h on 8xH200. On the same GPU and serving stack, the byte model’s gap to the original falls within the paired bootstrap interval on MATH-500, HumanEval+, MMLU-Pro and non-thinking GSM8K, and it loses 4-6 points on C-Eval, thinking-mode GSM8K and non-thinking IFEval. Without thinking it leads on every character-level task (CUTE 75.2 -> 84.0, EXECUTE-zho 81.6 -> 93.9, our Chinese character suite 54.4 -> 64.0), but lookup knowledge about Chinese characters such as pronunciation and stroke count drops, and thinking does not recover it. The cost is throughput: per-step speed is identical, but each step yields 3-5x less output; Chinese characters per second is about 1/4 of the original, and prefill at 64K bytes is 6.5x slower. We also document four byte-model failure modes (greedy runaway, sampling breaking UTF-8, ineffective penalty terms on a small vocabulary, repetition) and their fixes.

[13] OpticalRAG: Pixel-Space Compression for Token-Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Generation cs.CLPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 OpticalRAG，将文档渲染为页面图像并用冻结视觉编码器编码，通过文本查询检索视觉 token，再经查询驱动蒸馏压缩为仅 16 个注入 token 供 LLM 使用，从而缓解 RAG 的 token 预算瓶颈。方法包含编码器桥接检索和查询驱动蒸馏两个组件，无需昂贵的跨模态对比训练。

Details

Motivation: RAG 受限于 LLM 上下文 token 预算：长文档直接输入成本高，而文本压缩会不可逆地丢失细粒度信息。论文认为瓶颈源于压缩模态选择，而非信息密度本身，因此探索像素空间压缩以保留更高信息量。

Result: 在 LongBench-v2、BAMBOO 和 LooGLE-v2 上，OpticalRAG 仅注入 16 个 token 即取得最佳压缩感知准确率（CAP）；在 LongBench-v2 上使闭卷 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 主干模型提升 5.17 分。

Insight: 创新点在于将文档转为页面图像并以视觉 token 作为高密度压缩载体，同时用共享投影实现文本查询到冻结视觉特征空间的检索桥接，并用轻量 cross-attention 压缩器做查询条件蒸馏。其可借鉴之处是避免昂贵跨模态训练，在极低注入 token 预算下兼顾检索与压缩保真度。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is bottlenecked by the token budget of large language models: feeding long documents into the context window is expensive, while aggressive textual compression discards fine-grained information that is irreversibly lost in a one-dimensional token sequence. We argue that this bottleneck is not fundamental to information density but to the choice of compression \emph{modality}. Modern visual encoders can pack the contents of a rendered text page into roughly a hundred visual tokens with little semantic loss, opening a new compression-fidelity tradeoff that purely textual methods cannot reach. We present \textbf{OpticalRAG}, a framework that renders documents as page images, encodes them with a frozen vision encoder, and distills the retrieved visual tokens into as few as $16$ injected tokens before passing them to the LLM. Two ingredients make this work without expensive cross-modal training: (i) \emph{Encoder-Bridged Retrieval}, which projects text-query states into the visual feature space of the frozen encoder, through a projection shared with the compressor, to retrieve visual tokens from a text query, with no contrastive training; and (ii) \emph{Query-Driven Distillation}, a lightweight cross-attention compressor that condenses the retrieved visual tokens into a small set of query-conditioned prefix tokens. On LongBench-v2, BAMBOO, and LooGLE-v2, OpticalRAG achieves the best compression-aware accuracy (CAP) on all three benchmarks while injecting only $16$ tokens, and improves the closed-book Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbone by $5.17$ points on LongBench-v2.Code is available at https://github.com/brighterr/OpticalRAG.

[15] Sckwoky at SHROOM-Visions: Character-Level Hallucination-Span Detection in Image-Conditioned VLMs via Dual-Prompted LoRA Decoders cs.CLPDF

Mikhail Kulakov, Ruslan Solikhov, Ivan Bondarenko

TL;DR: 论文提出 SHROOM-Visions 2026 共享任务的参赛系统 Sckwoky，用于在英、法、意、中四种语言的图像条件 VLM 回答中做字符级幻觉片段检测。系统以冻结的 Gemma-4-12B-it 为骨干，仅训练约 10.7M 参数的 LoRA 适配器；为绕开因果解码器看不到后续 token 的问题，把候选答案在同一提示中重复一次，取第二次出现的隐状态，从而在单次非自回归前向中获得近似双向上下文。字符级定位器融合中间层隐状态，预测幻觉概率、BIO 边界和回答级弃答分数，再由各语言专用分类器为每个片段判定五种幻觉类型之一。封闭测试集上宏平均字符 IoU 达 0.4404，为官方基线（0.1854）的两倍多。

Details

Motivation: 图像条件 VLM 的回答常含幻觉，需要定位到字符级片段才能支持可信度提示或纠错；但直接用因果解码器做检测时单向注意力使每个位置看不到后续上下文，而另建独立验证器模型代价高、不利于本地部署。论文希望以少量可训练参数、复用同一生成模型权重，实现多语言字符级幻觉片段检测。

Result: 封闭测试集上宏平均字符 IoU 为 0.4404，超过官方基线 0.1854 两倍以上，四语种 IoU 排名第 5–6 位。英语调参集消融显示：答案重复带来的增益与 LoRA 适配相当（但留出集增益较小，且集中在含幻觉的回答上）；打乱图文配对后各语言 IoU 均降至“全预测无幻觉”基线或更低，说明检测确实依赖图文对应关系。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是用“双提示+答案重复”让单次非自回归前向获得近似双向上下文，并联合输出字符级定位、BIO 边界、弃答分数与各语言专用幻觉类型分类。客观来看最巧妙之处是用极简的重复输入技巧规避因果模型的结构限制、以语言特定分类器处理多语种，并可复用生成模型基座权重，工程实用性较强；不过增益有相当部分来自输入构造而非模型结构创新。

Abstract: We present our system for the SHROOM-visions 2026 shared task on character-level hallucination span detection in image-conditioned vision-language model (VLM) responses, evaluated across English, French, Italian, and Chinese. The detector is built on a frozen Gemma-4-12B-it backbone with trainable LoRA adapters (approximately 10.7M parameters). Because the backbone is a causal decoder, hidden states read from a single copy of an answer cannot attend to subsequent tokens; we therefore repeat the candidate answer within one prompt and extract features from its second occurrence, giving every position approximate bidirectional context in a single non-autoregressive pass. A character-level locator combines intermediate hidden states to predict hallucination probabilities, BIO boundaries, and a response-level abstention score, while separate language-specific classifiers assign one of five hallucination types to each predicted span. On the closed test set, our system reaches a macro-averaged character IoU of 0.4404, more than doubling the official baseline (0.1854), and ranks 5th-6th on IoU across the four languages. On the English tuning split, controlled ablations attribute a gain to the repeated answer that is comparable to that of LoRA adaptation (its held-out gain is smaller and concentrated on hallucinated answers), and they show that the detector relies on the image-answer pairing: shuffling images across examples drives IoU down to the level of the predict-nothing floor or below it in every language. In deployments where the same Gemma-4-12B-it model also generates answers, its base weights can be reused, making the approach practical for local question-answering systems that cannot run a separate verifier.

[16] StaRSE: Compact Russian Sentence Embeddings with a Sign-Coded Static Encoder cs.CL | cs.IRPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 StaRSE，一种面向俄语的紧凑静态句子编码器。它将词元方向存为符号位、词元范数单独存储，部署时通过范数缩放后的符号方向均值池化生成句子嵌入，从而完全移除 Transformer 推理。模型从 ruBERT-base 初始化，经俄语相邻句和任务形态句对微调，并用对比学习直接优化符号投影后的词元表。

Details

Motivation: Transformer 句子编码器检索质量强，但在 CPU 上生成嵌入或需频繁刷新嵌入时推理成本成为瓶颈，论文希望用极紧凑、低延迟的静态编码方案替代部署时的 Transformer 推理。

Result: 在 RuMTEB 上任务均值为 51.16，比最强静态基线高 7.98 分；打包模型仅 11.3 MiB，CPU 平均延迟 0.08 ms/句，而最接近质量的上下文编码器估计为 55.7 MiB（16-bit 参数）和 1.43 ms/句。

Insight: 创新点在于符号位存储词元方向并配合独立范数的静态句子编码，以及从 ruBERT-base 初始化后通过相邻句监督、任务形态句对和直接优化符号投影词元表的对比后训练来保持质量；客观看，其价值主要是面向 CPU/高频场景的极致压缩与低延迟部署方案。

Abstract: Transformer sentence encoders provide strong retrieval quality, but their inference cost becomes a bottleneck when embeddings must be produced on CPUs or refreshed frequently. We introduce StaRSE, a compact Russian static sentence encoder that removes Transformer inference at deployment time: token directions are stored as sign bits, token norms are stored separately, and sentences are encoded by mean pooling over norm-scaled sign directions. StaRSE is initialized from ruBERT-base, fine-tuned on Russian adjacent-sentence and task-shaped pairs, and post-trained with a contrastive stage that directly optimizes the sign-projected token table. On RuMTEB, StaRSE reaches a task mean of $51.16$, $7.98$ points above the strongest included static baseline. The packaged model occupies $11.3$ MiB and has a mean CPU latency of $0.08$ ms per sentence, compared with an estimated $55.7$ MiB 16-bit parameter footprint and $1.43$ ms mean CPU latency for the closest-quality contextual encoder. Ablations examine adjacent-sentence supervision, token norms, and direct sign-projected post-training.

[17] Dyship: Leveraging Acoustic Features For Speaker Relationship Classification From Dyadic Conversations cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Kieran Sturges, Thomas Thebaud, Yaohan Guan, Najim Dehak

TL;DR: 论文提出DyShip框架，在Seamless Interaction双人对话语料上，根据对话语音判断说话人之间的熟悉度和亲属关系。研究系统比较了频谱图、MFCC、WavLM自监督表示、x-vector和估计发音运动五类语音表示，发现频谱特征更适合熟悉度分类，WavLM在亲属关系分类上最优，x-vector在亲属关系上具有较高AUC。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注从双人对话中推断说话人关系（熟悉度和亲属关系）这一关系推理问题，旨在比较不同语音表示对该任务的有效性，并探索对话中的声学线索。

Result: 频谱特征在熟悉度分类上达到87.81%准确率；WavLM在亲属关系分类上达到85.17%最高准确率；x-vector在亲属关系分类上AUC达0.909，发音特征在熟悉度上较弱但在亲属关系上有效；多数分类器超过同一数据集上先前报告的人类和音频语言模型分数。

Insight: 创新点在于系统比较多种传统声学特征与自监督表示在说话人关系分类中的表现，并揭示传统频谱特征和x-vector等表示在关系推断中的潜力；客观看，其价值主要来自对语音表示与关系线索的实证分析，而非提出复杂新模型。

Abstract: We introduce DyShip, a framework for classifying speaker familiarity and kinship from dyadic conversations in the Seamless Interaction corpus. We compare five speech representations: spectrograms, MFCCs, self-supervised WavLM representations, x-vectors, and estimated articulatory movements. Spectral features perform best for familiarity classification, reaching an accuracy of 87.81%, while WavLM achieves the highest kinship accuracy of 85.17%. X-vectors and articulatory features yield weaker results on familiarity classifiaction, but shine for kinship classification, with an AUC of 0.909 for x-vectors. Most classifiers exceed previously reported human and audio language model scores on the same dataset. These findings highlight the potential of conventional acoustic features for relationship inference and motivate further investigation of the conversational cues captured by different representations.

[18] An AI Assistant for Automating Anti-Corruption Expertise of Draft Legislation Based on the TRACE Framework cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Serhii Zabolotnii, Alina Tarasova

TL;DR: 论文提出一个基于TRACE架构的AI助手，用于对立法草案进行反腐败审查：定位专家可能质疑的条款并指出监管缺陷。系统由确定性解析核心、模型引导的单条款定性、基于规则的因素分配和外部人工监督组成；作者还用乌克兰NAZK公开结论构建了132部法律草案、474条模型抽取发现的开放基准进行评估。

Details

Motivation: 立法草案的反腐败审查通常依赖专家人工，效率低且难以规模化；论文希望自动化筛查腐败风险条款并给出缺陷类型，辅助而非替代专家审查。

Result: 在100部草案上，系统前10个标记对发现的匹配率为0.28（本地Gemma 4 31B）和0.30（Sonnet 5.5），全标记匹配率为0.43–0.46（非盲测），高于冻结盲基线0.21；在21部留出法案上严格指标优于包含完整方法论的单一提示，但宽松指标未优，Opus 5.5相对Sonnet无增益。

Insight: 创新点在于将确定性解析、模型单条款判断、规则化因素分配和人工监督组合成可解释流水线，并基于NAZK结论构建反腐败审查基准；客观看，其价值主要在领域流程拆解与评估设置，但结果仍依赖模型抽取发现和LLM裁判，且缺少人类专家验证。

Abstract: We present an AI assistant for automating anti-corruption expertise of draft legislation based on the TRACE architecture, designed to screen bills for corruption risks: pinpoint provisions an expert would criticise and name the regulatory defect in each. The pipeline combines a deterministic parsing core, model-guided single-provision qualification, rule-based factor assignment, and external human oversight. To evaluate the assistant, we constructed an open benchmark from the published conclusions of Ukraine’s National Agency on Corruption Prevention (NAZK), comprising 132 draft laws with 474 model-extracted findings. A system flag agrees with a finding when it targets a compatible provision and an LLM judge rates the risk as the same (strict) or partly the same (lenient). On 100 draft laws, the pipeline matches 0.28 of the findings within its top ten flags using the local Gemma 4 31B model, 0.30 with Sonnet 5.5, and 0.43-0.46 across all flags (non-blind; the frozen blind baseline achieves 0.21, 95% CI 0.16-0.26). Across all runs, most misses occur on provisions that the system located but qualified as a different risk, and none of the 32 legal-collision findings was matched. On 21 acts held out from tuning, the pipeline outperforms a monolithic prompt containing the full methodology under the strict measure, though not under the lenient one, and Opus 5.5 adds nothing over Sonnet. The reported scores reflect agreement with model-extracted findings rated by an LLM judge whose two access routes agree on 73 of 74 labels, without human expert validation.

[19] Attribute-Evidence Learning with Multimodal Prompt for Generalized Category Discovery cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Wei Zhang, Zhu Teng, Zhixiang Chi, Junnan Zou, Wenxin Luo

TL;DR: 论文提出AptM-GCD多模态框架，用于广义类别发现（GCD）。该方法通过属性证据学习（AEL）将属性作为局部视觉证据，与patch级视觉token交互以提取可靠判别区域，再利用多模态提示学习（MPL）把细粒度文本证据转为视觉提示并注入多模态提示Transformer，从而增强新旧类别判别。

Details

Motivation: 现有GCD方法多依赖视觉线索，或虽引入类别标签和属性等文本信息，却将全局标签与局部属性纠缠在共享全局图像表示中，导致属性不可靠并可能误导预测。论文旨在更可靠地利用文本属性指导开放类别发现。

Result: 在六个数据集上的实验表明，AptM-GCD在细粒度和通用GCD基准上持续达到SoTA，并在单模态与类不平衡GCD设置下取得一致增益，展现出较强泛化能力。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点在于将属性视为局部视觉证据而非全局类别描述符，并通过属性嵌入与patch级token交互过滤噪声；同时用视觉提示注入实现局部属性语义到全局视觉理解的桥接，避免强制跨模态全局对齐。客观看，其可借鉴之处是解耦属性与全局表示、以局部证据和提示推理方式引入多模态信息。

Abstract: Generalized category discovery (GCD) aims to discover novel categories from unlabeled data by leveraging limited labeled samples from old classes. Existing methods that rely solely on visual cues often neglect the multimodal nature of human perception, prompting recent approaches to incorporate textual information such as category tags and attributes. However, these approaches typically entangle global tags and local attributes within a shared global image representation, rendering attributes unreliable and potentially providing misleading cues that can drive the model toward incorrect predictions. To this end, we propose AptM-GCD, a novel multimodal framework that exploits apt textual guidance through attribute evidence learning and multimodal prompt reasoning. Specifically, we design an Attribute-Evidence Learning (AEL) mechanism that treats attributes as localized visual evidence rather than global category descriptors. By interacting attribute embeddings with patch-level visual tokens, AEL identifies reliable attribute-related regions and extracts discriminative local evidence, thereby reducing the impact of noisy or irrelevant attribute cues. Building upon the extracted evidence, we further develop a Multimodal Prompt Learning (MPL) mechanism to bridge local attribute semantics and global visual understanding. MPL converts fine-grained textual evidence into visual prompts and injects them into a multimodal prompt transformer, enabling attribute information to enhance category discrimination through contextual reasoning without enforcing direct global alignment between modalities. Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate that AptM-GCD consistently achieves SoTA performance across both fine-grained and generic benchmarks. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits strong generalization ability, delivering consistent gains under unimodal and class-imbalanced GCD settings.

[20] Partition, Refine, and Detect: Preserving Novel Category Capacity in Open-Vocabulary Object Detection cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Wei Zhang, Zhu Teng, Zhixiang Chi, Junnan Zou, Yi Liu

TL;DR: 论文提出PR-OVD，一种面向开放词汇目标检测的分区-精炼框架，用于显式保留新类别的表示容量并增强类别分离。该方法在视觉和文本模态中粗分区基类、新类和显式背景，并进行类别级精炼，同时设计结构提示为新类提供可靠视觉锚点。实验表明其可提升新类检测性能，并可集成到不同检测器架构中。

Details

Motivation: 现有开放词汇检测依赖CLIP式视觉-语言嵌入，但表示空间偏向基类，新类有效容量不足，易导致新语义被压缩、语义坍缩及推理置信度校准不佳。论文旨在缓解基类主导表示问题，显式为新类保留容量并增强类别可分性。

Result: 在两个开放词汇目标检测基准上，PR-OVD相较SOTA方法在新类类别上取得平均6.5%的比例提升。该方法还收益于开放词汇分割和分类任务，验证了有效性与通用性。

Insight: 创新点在于将基类、新类和显式背景的粗分区与类别级精炼结合，防止无关嵌入泄漏并锐化决策边界，同时用认知启发的结构提示编码自我中心属性与异中心上下文，为新类提供稳定视觉锚点。客观来看，其可插拔式设计及跨任务迁移验证增强了方法的通用性和借鉴价值。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) expects the model trained on base classes to recognize both base and novel classes at inference. Typical methods rely on CLIP-style vision-language embeddings, yet the learned space is often biased toward base categories, with limited effective capacity preserved for novel concepts. Such a base-dominated representation can compress novel semantics, leading to semantic collapse and mis-calibrated confidence at inference. To address this issue, we present PR-OVD, a partition-and-refine framework that explicitly reserves capacity for novel categories and strengthens category separation, while being seamlessly integrated into different detector architectures. Specifically, PR-OVD first performs coarse partitioning to build global divisions for base, novel, and an explicit background partition in both visual and textual modalities, preventing object-irrelevant embeddings from leaking into category-specific representations. It then applies category-level refinement within each partition to sharpen decision boundaries and alleviate the lack of fine-grained region-level pretraining in CLIP. To further provide reliable visual anchors for novel categories, we design a cognitively inspired Structure Prompt (SP) that encodes both egocentric attributes and allocentric context, facilitating stable partition-and-refine learning. Extensive experiments on two OVD benchmarks show that PR-OVD delivers an average 6.5% proportional improvement on novel categories over the state-of-the-art methods. Notably, PR-OVD also benefits other open-vocabulary tasks, including segmentation and classification, further confirming its effectiveness and generality.

[21] Selective Surgical Action-Center Localization:Retaining Localization Gains through Supervised Scoring cs.CVPDF

Yanchong Xue, Wanting Zhang, Lingtao Yu, Guochao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文研究手术动作中心定位中的“选择性定位”问题：不仅需要定位准确，还要判断是否报告该中心。作者提出结合热图与坐标监督的定位器，并训练逻辑回归评分器筛选出有可报告中心且定位误差在容差内的预测。在5,000帧、50个手术来源的内部评估中，该方法在保留正确输出的指标上优于基线。

Details

Motivation: 当包含和不包含可报告中心的帧混合时，定位性能提升是否能真正保留下来，取决于选择规则会保留哪些正确候选、拒绝哪些正确候选。论文旨在避免因选择机制错误拒绝正确预测，从而侵蚀定位改进带来的收益。

Result: 在每来源80%接受配额、0.10倍图像对角线容差下，该方法每100个输入保留46.08个正确输出，基线定位器配合相同评分方法为44.20，提升1.88（95% source-paired bootstrap区间0.94到2.79）。对坐标监督定位器，监督评分将正确预测被拒绝数从每100输入6.15降至2.99；基于熵的选择则因正确预测被更多拒绝而抵消平均正确供给增加。

Insight: 创新点在于将定位与是否报告的决策显式结合，用监督评分器学习识别“有可报告中心且误差在容差内”的预测，并通过评估保留的正确输出来衡量选择规则是否保留或侵蚀定位改进。客观看，其价值在于把选择性预测/拒识机制引入手术动作中心定位，并强调选择阶段对最终有效性能的影响。

Abstract: Surgical action-center localization requires both an accurate reference point and a decision about whether to report it. When frames with and without a reportable center are mixed, the benefit of improved localization depends on which correct candidates survive selection. We present a selective localization pipeline combining heatmap and coordinate supervision with a logistic-regression scorer. The scorer learns to identify predictions with a reportable center and a localization error within a specified tolerance. The internal evaluation uses 5,000 frames from 50 surgical sources, five source-grouped folds and two random seeds. Matched locator and scoring comparisons track correct candidates and their rejection. At an 80% acceptance quota per source and a tolerance of 0.10 times the image diagonal, our method retains 46.08 correct outputs per 100 inputs. The baseline locator with the same scoring approach retains 44.20. The gain is 1.88 (descriptive 95% source-paired bootstrap interval, 0.94 to 2.79). Under entropy-based selection, increased rejection of correct predictions offsets the higher supply of correct predictions on average. For the coordinate-supervised locator, supervised scoring reduces these rejections from 6.15 to 2.99 per 100 inputs. Evaluating retained correct outputs reveals whether a selection rule preserves or erodes an improvement in localization.

[22] Trajectory-Regularized Distribution Distillation for One-Step Generation cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

An Zhao, Yixiang Zhou, Zejian Li, Yi Guo, Pei Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出轨迹正则化分布蒸馏（TRD），用于将flow matching模型蒸馏为一步生成器。TRD通过学生单次前向预测的动量混合策略，同时充当一步生成器、学生边缘速度估计器，以及沿教师ODE轨迹监督的闭式时变速度场，从而融合分布级与轨迹级监督。

Details

Motivation: 现有flow matching一步蒸馏中，轨迹对齐在小采样预算下性能下降，而分布匹配通常需要额外网络估计反向KL梯度中的学生score，增加训练成本。论文试图统一两类监督并避免第二可训练网络。

Result: 在SANA-1.6B和Stable Diffusion 3.5上，TRD在一步和少步生成中的生成质量与训练效率优于代表性一致性蒸馏和分布匹配基线，但样本多样性存在折衷；摘要未给出具体定量指标。

Insight: 创新点是用一个动量混合策略在单次学生前向中同时承担生成、边缘速度估计和轨迹正则化，从而将分布匹配与教师ODE轨迹监督结合并省去额外score网络。可借鉴之处在于用统一策略参数化多种蒸馏角色，以降低训练复杂度并提升小步数生成稳定性。

Abstract: Distilling flow matching models for one-step generation generally follows two paradigms. Trajectory alignment supervises the student with the teacher’s probability flow ODE, but its performance degrades under very small sampling budgets. Distribution matching directly optimizes the generated distribution, but estimating its reverse Kullback-Leibler gradient typically requires a separate network to track the evolving student score. We propose trajectory-regularized distribution distillation (TRD), which combines distribution-level and trajectory-level supervision through a momentum-mixture policy predicted in a single student forward pass. This policy serves simultaneously as a one-step generator, an estimator of the student’s marginal velocity that removes the need for a second trainable network in distribution matching, and a closed-form time-dependent velocity field supervised along the teacher ODE trajectory. Experiments on SANA-1.6B and Stable Diffusion 3.5 show that TRD outperforms representative consistency and distribution matching baselines in generation quality and training efficiency for both one-step and few-step generation, with a trade-off in sample diversity.

[23] Evidence-Grounded Iterative Reasoning for the Diagnosis of Diffuse Cystic Lung Diseases cs.CVPDF

Haoqing Li, Jun Shi, Tou Li, Xianmeng Chen, Rui Wang

TL;DR: 论文针对弥漫性囊性肺疾病（DCLDs）影像表现重叠、标注数据有限导致鉴别诊断困难的问题，提出DCLDs-EIR证据接地迭代推理框架。该方法利用肺与囊分割的多视图渲染初始化诊断假设，再以竞争性假设指导轴位CT局部检查，并结合临床信息从疾病特异知识图谱中检索证据，通过支持与反驳联合评估不断修订诊断状态，形成证据获取与假设验证的反馈闭环。

Details

Motivation: DCLDs的影像表现相互重叠，鉴别诊断困难，且标注数据有限制约监督模型发展；现有MLLM虽能整合影像与临床信息，但存在体积感知不完整和疾病特异证据支撑不足的问题。

Result: 多中心评估表明，DCLDs-EIR在诊断准确率上持续优于基线方法，并能通过诊断假设、患者观察与疾病知识之间的显式链接实现可追溯的鉴别诊断。

Insight: 创新点在于将诊断假设细化与多模态证据获取、验证耦合为迭代反馈闭环，并引入分割多视图渲染、知识图谱检索和证据支持/反驳机制。客观看，其可追溯性和证据接地设计有助于提升临床诊断推理的可解释性与可靠性。

Abstract: Overlapping imaging manifestations complicate the differential diagnosis of Diffuse Cystic Lung Diseases (DCLDs), while limited annotated data constrain supervised model development. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can integrate imaging and clinical information, but their diagnostic reasoning remains limited by incomplete volumetric perception and insufficient grounding in disease-specific evidence. We propose DCLDs-EIR, an evidence-grounded iterative reasoning framework coupling diagnostic hypothesis refinement with multimodal evidence acquisition and verification. Multiview renderings of predicted lung and cyst segmentations characterize global cyst distributions and initialize the differential diagnosis. Competing hypotheses guide axial CT inspection to identify local findings that, together with clinical context, inform retrieval from a disease-specific knowledge graph. Accumulated patient observations and retrieved knowledge are jointly assessed for diagnostic support and refutation to revise the diagnostic state. Updated hypotheses and unresolved distinctions guide subsequent inspection, forming a feedback loop between evidence acquisition and hypothesis verification. Multicenter evaluation demonstrates that DCLDs-EIR consistently improves diagnostic accuracy over baselines and enables traceable differential diagnosis through explicit links between diagnostic hypotheses, patient observations, and disease-specific knowledge.

[24] TIB: Sample-wise Tempered Information Bottleneck for Multimodal Attribution beyond Alignment Assumption cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.MMPDF

Qiming Huang, Peixi Liu, Jianbo Jiao

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态（视觉-语言）归因问题，指出现有基于信息瓶颈的归因方法通常假设图文对语义对齐，并在真实数据中部分错配时导致后验过度收缩和强制解释。为此提出样本级温度化信息瓶颈框架TIB，根据跨模态可靠性自适应调节归因强度，从而在不假设语义对齐的情况下实现多模态归因。

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态归因方法常把成对模态当作代理监督，隐含假设图文语义对齐；现实数据存在部分语义错配和不可靠跨模态对应，导致基于信息瓶颈的归因目标产生结构性缺陷。论文旨在解决该对齐假设被违背时的归因失效问题。

Result: 摘要在受控基准和大规模数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了所提TIB方法的有效性，并验证了所识别的失败模式；未给出具体定量指标或明确SOTA声明。

Insight: 创新点在于通过理论分析揭示IB归因在跨模态错配下的后验过度收缩与强制解释问题，并提出样本级温度化信息瓶颈机制，按跨模态可靠性自适应调制归因强度，且不依赖语义对齐假设；客观看，其贡献主要是问题诊断与可靠性自适应归因框架的结合。

Abstract: Multimodal (e.g. vision-language, in this work) attribution aims to interpret the models without access to ground-truth explanatory supervision. Existing multimodal attribution methods typically rely on paired modalities as proxy supervision, implicitly assuming that image-text pairs are semantically aligned. Information bottleneck (IB)-based attribution methods follow this general paradigm and instantiate it through a global sufficiency-compression objective. In realistic multimodal datasets, however, this assumption is frequently violated due to partial semantic misalignment and unreliable cross-modal correspondences. Through theoretical analysis, we show that, under such misalignment, information-bottleneck-based attribution objectives exhibit a structural limitation: by assuming equal reliability across all proxy supervision signals, they induce posterior over-contraction and forced explanations. To address this issue, we propose TIB, a sample-wise Tempered Information Bottleneck framework that adaptively modulates attribution strength according to cross-modal reliability, without assuming semantic alignment. Extensive experiments on controlled benchmarks and large-scale datasets show the effectiveness of the proposed approach, while also validating the identified failure mode.

[25] PhysMoE: Fast Spatial Grounding with Single-Token Coordinate Representation in Modality-Decoupled VLMs cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.ROPDF

Ruimeng Liu, Denan Liang, Ruofei Bai, Fuming Zhang, Kuan Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出PhysMoE，一种面向具身视觉语言模型的空间定位方法，通过模态解耦的专家路由、单Token坐标表示和可微SDF监督，解决离散Token化导致空间不连续与解码慢、标准点回归难以处理多接触可供性和不规则目标的问题。在9个坐标定位基准上相较基模型平均提升15.7%、吞吐加速8.31倍，并在11个文本具身基准上保持多模态理解与推理能力。

Details

Motivation: 现有具身VLM在grounding坐标预测中存在两类瓶颈：离散Token化破坏空间连续性且解码延迟高，标准点回归难以处理多接触可供性和不规则形状目标。因此需要兼顾空间连续性、解码效率和复杂几何建模的坐标预测方案。

Result: 在9个基于坐标的grounding基准（轨迹、可供性、定位）上，相较基模型平均提升15.7%，吞吐加速8.31倍；在11个文本具身基准上保持多模态理解与推理。摘要未明确宣称达到SOTA。

Insight: 创新点包括按物理模态解耦上下层并路由到空间/语言专家以消除梯度干扰；复用视觉编码器位置嵌入将每个坐标编码为单Token并注入2D空间先验；用可微SDF监督指向点，无需昂贵Mask解码器即可处理可供性与不规则几何。客观看，将坐标表示与视觉位置先验对齐并用SDF监督建模复杂几何，是兼顾效率与空间精度的可借鉴思路。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are backbones for robotic planning and manipulation. However, for coordinate prediction in grounding tasks, existing architectures suffer from two distinct bottlenecks: discrete tokenization disrupts spatial continuity with high decoding latency, while standard point regression fails on multi-contact affordances and irregularly shaped targets. To resolve these challenges, we propose PhysMoE, introducing three core contributions: (1) sharing lower layers for multimodal context while routing upper layers to specialized spatial (pixel) and linguistic (text) experts by physical modality, eliminating gradient interference; (2) encoding each coordinate as a single token by reusing the visual encoder’s learned positional embeddings, injecting 2D spatial priors to natively align coordinates with visual features and ease training; and (3) differentiable Signed Distance Field (SDF) supervision on pointing, accommodating affordances and irregular geometries without costly mask decoders. Compared to the base model, PhysMoE achieves a 15.7% average gain and an 8.31x throughput speedup across 9 coordinate-based grounding benchmarks (trajectory, affordance, and localization), while preserving multimodal understanding and reasoning across 11 text-based embodied benchmarks.

[26] Instance-preserving graded semantic reporting for surgical action triplets cs.CVPDF

Yanchong Xue, Wanting Zhang, Lingtao Yu, Guochao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文针对手术动作三元组（器械-动词-目标）识别中”完整报告或拒绝”策略会丢弃正确部分预测的问题，提出实例保持的分级语义报告方法。该方法将实例选择与语义粒度选择解耦，每个被选中的实例保留其预测掩码和标签，由分层正确性评分引导按器械（I）、器械-动词（IV）或完整三元组（IVT）级别进行报告，并用效用增量规则DELTA在更细粒度报告的预期收益与错误代价之间权衡。

Details

Motivation: 传统完整或拒绝（complete-or-reject）的报告方式在目标不确定时会丢弃已正确识别的器械和动作，导致正确的部分预测信息被浪费，需要一种能在不确定时仍保留部分正确信息的报告机制。

Result: 在8个手术视频的5,641个测试帧上，于10%开发校准目标下，DELTA规则保留了被匹配的complete-or-reject基线拒绝的候选中80.0%（5,329/6,662）的正确部分报告；加入IV级别后，三种规则相对分别重校准的I/IVT对照组使源归一化加权正确报告产出每100帧提升19.13至23.13，加权报告风险变化为-0.77至+1.01个百分点，完整正确三元组计数保持不变或略有下降。

Insight: 创新点在于将实例选择与语义细节层级解耦、引入分层正确性评分以支持I/IV/IVT多粒度报告，并用DELTA效用增量规则显式权衡报告效用与完整性；从客观角度看，其价值在于让部分手术预测可作为局部化描述被利用，并量化了报告效用与完整性的权衡关系。

Abstract: Complete-or-reject reporting of surgical action triplets can discard a correctly identified instrument and action when the target is uncertain. We introduce instance-preserving graded semantic reporting, which separates instance selection from the choice of semantic detail. Each selected instance retains its predicted mask and labels while hierarchical correctness scores guide instrument (I), instrument-verb (IV), or complete instrument-verb-target (IVT) reporting. The utility-increment rule DELTA weighs the expected gain from finer reporting against its error cost. We compare three decision rules on 5,641 test frames from eight surgical videos using fixed predictions and scores. At the 10% development calibration target, DELTA retains correct partial reports for 80.0% of candidates rejected by a matched complete-or-reject baseline (5,329/6,662). Adding IV increases source-normalized weighted correct-report yield by 19.13 to 23.13 per 100 frames across all three rules relative to separately recalibrated I/IVT controls. Weighted reporting risk changes by -0.77 to +1.01 percentage points, with complete correct-triplet counts unchanged or reduced. Graded reporting makes partial surgical predictions available as localized descriptions, with an explicit trade-off between report utility and completeness.

Abstract: Precise multimodal retinal image rigid registration (MRIRG) is essential for aligning cross-modal vessel structures, offering critical guidance for ophthalmic surgery. Inspired by the above analysis, we propose a novel One-step Frequency-Structure Similarity Registration (OFS2Reg) learning framework for MRIRG that leverages the potential of cross-modal vessel structure similarities to construct keypoint topology constraints under a shared detection network. To be specific, OFS2Reg applies a well-designed frequency-guided structure preservation descriptor to construct coarse keypoint topology constraints in descriptors by highlighting significant vessel structures. Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are typically evaluated by whether they produce correct answers, but correctness alone does not reveal whether their outputs are supported by the right visual evidence. To construct VIGOR at scale, we develop an automatic annotation framework that reuses existing dense supervision when available and generates missing concept groundings through proposal-based annotation followed by conservative multimodal verification. Using VIGOR, we evaluate whether MLLMs “look at what they say” by comparing model-derived visual evidence for each expressed concept against annotated ground-truth rationales. Details

Motivation: 现有文生图模型能生成逼真模拟时钟却不能控制其显示时间，且请求特定圆形比例时也失败；这些失败共享角度变量参数化的几何结构，论文旨在实现连续角度可控生成。

Result: 在模拟时钟上，方法达到63-78% Acc@6°，基线低于1%；在圆形比例生成上达到89.5% Acc@0.10，未见物体类别88.8%、未见目标值84.9%，预训练基线约20%。

Insight: 创新点包括：用周期正弦-余弦特征编码角度并转为角色特定token；通过SLR在训练中从部件级到对象级再到无约束定位随机放松注意力监督；用时钟手交换偏好目标避免正确角度分配给错误指针角色。客观看，核心是将角度控制转化为角色化几何token条件，并以训练期监督退火降低推理时定位依赖。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can render a photorealistic analog clock but cannot control the time it displays. Across four pretrained models, fewer than 1% of generated clocks place both hands within 6 degrees of the requested time; the models instead collapse onto visually dominant configurations such as the canonical 10:10 pose. The same failure appears when a specific fraction of a circular object is requested, where models return complete or conventionally sliced objects. We observe that these two failures share a geometric structure: both target quantities can be parameterized by angular variables. We introduce a geometric token encoding framework that conditions generation directly on these angles. Each angular component is encoded as a periodic sine-cosine feature and projected into a role-specific token embedding that is injected into the prompt before a frozen text encoder. To spatially ground these tokens without requiring localization input at inference, we introduce Stochastic Localization Relaxation (SLR), which stochastically relaxes attention supervision from component-level to object-level to unconstrained localization during training. For clocks, we additionally introduce a hand-swap preference objective that discourages assigning the correct angles to the wrong hand roles. On analog clocks our method reaches 63-78% Acc@6 degrees across evaluation methods, compared with below 1% for the baselines. On circular-fraction generation, it achieves 89.5% Acc@0.10, while maintaining 88.8% on unseen object categories and 84.9% on unseen target values, compared with roughly 20% for pretrained baselines.

Motivation: 缩放视图能定位小目标但可能丢失识别所需上下文，全屏视图保留上下文但对小目标较弱；需要根据预测一致性决定何时引入第二模型，以兼顾准确率与调用成本。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding maps a screenshot and a natural-language instruction to the screen location where an agent should act. Zoomed views can reveal small targets but may lose the context needed to identify them. We introduce GroundCascade, a training-free controller that uses agreement between a grounder’s full-screen and zoomed predictions to decide when to consult a complementary model. It selects between zoomed predictions while preserving corroborated full-screen locations. An optional Finalizer predicts from an unmarked joint crop when disagreement remains (Full); omitting it yields the two-stage Selective variant. GroundCascade reaches 85.33% on ScreenSpot-Pro with Indeed-UI-32B/8B/32B and 88.43% on the visible-target OSWorld-G-Refined subset with Qwen3.6-27B/3.8-27B/3.8-27B, gains of 2.66 and 2.35 percentage points over the stronger standalone two-pass grounder. On UI-Vision, GroundCascade reaches 72.20% category-balanced accuracy, with Selective slightly higher at 72.28%. Across these three evaluations, GroundCascade in Full mode uses 2.42-2.73 calls per request, 31-39% fewer than unconditionally acquiring both models’ two-pass predictions. At matched second-model call budgets, selective acquisition gains 1.52-2.56 points over standalone Two-pass, compared with 0.22-0.69 points from stratified random acquisition. GroundCascade leads the official ScreenSpot-Pro leaderboard and exceeds reported GUI-specialist comparisons on OSWorld-G-Refined and UI-Vision, subject to differences in external evaluation protocols.

Details

Insight: 创新在于把“评审”和“编辑”通过六类问题体系与“发现→对应编辑工具”的路由解耦连接，且该体系不依赖具体生成模型；以高召回多模态评审模型扩大人工标记范围、人工补充模型遗漏，形成互补协作；同时开源可复用的评审门户模板和带人工标注与基线分数的数据集，便于后续研究复现。

Abstract: Generative pipelines can now turn every product page of an e-commerce catalog into a short video ad, but deciding whether such an ad is usable is not one good-or-bad call. We describe how we operationalized the review and editing of these ads so that both can keep pace with generation: reviewers go through each ad scene by scene and answer six questions, on the product, scene quality, pronunciation, script, sound and captions; judges built from vision-language, speech and language models file each finding under the matching question, and reviewers accept or reject each finding and add what the judges missed. Each confirmed issue then opens an edit tool matched to it. The questions and the routing from a finding to its fix do not depend on the models that generate the ad. Across 120 ads from our pipeline, reviewed with judge findings on screen, reviewers marked the product wrong on 60% of scenes, and script, caption or sound problems on under 10% each. On 60 of these ads, they marked the product wrong on 58% of scenes, far more than the 26% marked by two reviewers who later saw the ads with the judges hidden; one reviewer who saw the ads both ways marked 57% with judge findings and 19% without. On 120 blind-labeled scenes, a product judge that watches the clip with the catalog photos reached AUC 0.84, while video-quality and photo-similarity scores were close to chance. We tune the judges for recall: the product judge catches nearly all defects but also flags many clean scenes, so reviewers decide; together, the judges widen what reviewers mark and reviewers add what the judges miss. Without judge findings, one reviewer’s marking faded over a session. We release a review-portal template and our review data (120 ads and 562 scenes with judge findings and human marks, plus 120 blind-labeled scenes with baseline scores) at https://github.com/anoubhav/ad-review-and-edit.

Details

Motivation: 织物交易中买家需检查每英寸纱线数，但无实验室仪器时只能通过挑纱镜人工慢速计数。智能手机拍照有望加速检查并留下记录，因此需要判断此类照片是否足以支撑可靠计数，并建立面向该任务的图像质量与评测基准。

Result: 在 PickGlass-TPI 上，无方法达到±5%容差；最佳 Molmo2-4B（仅用合成图调优）误差约25%，与最佳前沿模型 GPT-6.1-sol（直接提问27%，给四张放大近景25%）相当，均优于非专家标注员37%。PG-Lattice 跨两张同织物照片计数最常落在5%内，但失误常达两倍；参考计数跨重拍中位重复性也仅9%，说明单张照片尚不能证明满足±5%。通用无参考画质分数不能排序 PG-Lattice 误差，生成式增强提升感知分数但不能可靠改善计数。

Insight: 创新点在于将图像质量定义为织物密度计数任务上的可用性和跨重拍可靠性，并构建带纺织受训参考标注的 PickGlass-TPI 基准；同时验证小规模开源VLM仅用合成图调优即可接近前沿模型。客观看，可借鉴的是任务驱动评测、重拍一致性指标，以及对通用画质分数和生成增强在专业测量任务中局限性的实证分析。

Abstract: Thread density, the threads per inch along and across a woven fabric, is a basic specification that buyers check whenever fabric is sold. Without laboratory instruments, inspectors count it slowly, by eye, through a pick glass, a folding 10$\times$ magnifier with a one-inch window. A smartphone photo through that glass could make the check faster and leave a record. We judge such a photo by what it is used for, as in task-based image quality: it is good if the thread count from it is within $\pm$5% of the true count, a common tolerance in fabric specifications. We introduce PickGlass-TPI: 76 factory scenes of 44 fabric patches, each counted in an open annotation tool by a textile-trained annotator, whose counts are the reference. On it we benchmark frontier and open vision-language models, CNNs, counting specialists and a training-free spectral counter (PG-Lattice). No method agrees with the reference within the tolerance. The best methods are off by about a quarter: a small open model that we tuned only on synthetic images (Molmo2-4B, 25%) is level with the best frontier model, GPT-6.1-sol (27% when asked directly, 25% given four magnified close-ups of the window). Both err less than a non-expert annotator (37%), but only the tuned model also beats, on both error measures, a baseline that guesses each scene’s density as the average of the others. PG-Lattice most often repeats its count within 5% across two photos of the same patch, but its misses, like GPT-6.1-sol’s errors, are often a factor of two. Even the reference repeats a count across such re-shoots only to a median 9%, so one photo cannot yet be shown to meet $\pm$5%; the image quality that matters is reliability across re-shoots. Generic no-reference quality scores chosen before labeling do not rank PG-Lattice’s error, and generative enhancement raises perceptual scores but does not reliably improve the count.

Abstract: Temporal action segmentation (TAS) is commonly trained on large banks of pre-extracted frame features, but storing and replaying those features becomes a practical bottleneck for long-form video, especially in incremental TAS where past-task data must remain available to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. Recent TAS replay and condensation methods reduce this burden, but they rely on dense frame-wise annotations and allocate storage through fixed segment-level structures, limiting their flexibility for long procedural videos whose frame complexity varies substantially over time. We introduce Adaptive Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (ARQ-VAE), a compact and label-free memory representation for TAS features that learns a discrete residual quantization of frame-level video features and then adaptively truncates the residual chain on a per-frame basis, retaining only the residual prefix that best reconstructs each frame feature. This yields a practical compact feature memory that does not depend on frame-wise labels and can therefore support multiple TAS settings with the same stored representation, while a lightweight fine-tuning stage further improves reconstruction quality for the truncated codes that are actually retained. Across standard TAS benchmarks, ARQ-VAE achieves a substantially improved storage-performance trade-off over prior replay and condensation baselines. On Breakfast, our final representation reduces training-set storage from 28 GB for the original I3D feature bank to 10 MB while retaining strong downstream segmentation performance; with ASFormer, it achieves 71.5 Edit and 51.2 F1@50 in the supervised setting. We further show that the same label-free memory remains effective for incremental and unsupervised TAS. We position ARQ-VAE as a practical memory representation for TAS and a promising direction for broader dataset condensation applications.

Despite significant progress driven by transformer-based models, existing TSG methods suffer from two key limitations: quantization errors due to temporal downsampling, which misalign segment boundaries, and suboptimal ranking of predictions based on semantic matching rather than localization precision. To address these challenges, we propose the Adaptive Boundary Refinement and Ranking (ABRR) framework, a novel, model-agnostic post-processing approach that enhances the performance of any TSG model without retraining. ABRR integrates three innovative components: (1) Temporal Distribution Smoothing, which mitigates noise in predicted score distributions using Gaussian kernel convolution; (2) Boundary Refinement via Optimal Transport, which leverages the 2-Wasserstein distance and Sinkhorn iterations to align coarse segment boundaries with true temporal moments, grounded in Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport theory; and (3) Quality-Driven Re-Ranking, which reorders predictions using a composite score balancing localization precision and semantic relevance. By correcting quantization errors and prioritizing accurately localized segments, ABRR achieves substantial improvements over state-of-the-art TSG models. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets, including Charades-STA, ActivityNet Captions, and QVHighlights, demonstrate consistent gains at stringent IoU thresholds (e.g., IoU=0.7), alongside enhanced ranking performance. The framework’s computational efficiency and compatibility with existing models make it a versatile and impactful solution for real-world TSG applications.

Abstract: Accurate localization of abnormalities in three-dimensional (3D) CT scans is essential for linking automated predictions with clinically relevant pathological findings. A token-to-voxel upsampling module enables high-resolution localization, while gradient-guided thresholding identifies pathology regions without additional learned parameters. Evaluated on five localization tasks comprising 355 annotated CT scans, Uni-Loc achieves macro-averaged voxel-level AUROC/AUPR of 96.1%/34.5%, outperforming relevance propagation (80.7%/13.9%) and gradient saliency (88.7%/8.1%). [42] How Hyperbolic Are Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models? cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Freek Byrman, Partha Das, Stijn Verdenius, Pascal Mettes

TL;DR: 论文系统检验双曲视觉语言模型（VLMs）在实践中到底有多“双曲”。作者分析曲率进入网络的唯一操作——指数映射，发现MERU、HyCoCLIP、ARGENT和PHyCLIP中的指数映射近似恒等函数，推理时移除它几乎不影响评分；进一步用相同层级损失从零训练欧式VLM，在CC3M上其训练统计、评估分数和径向分布与双曲对应模型几乎一致。

Details

Motivation: 双曲VLMs被宣称具有强零样本性能和按一般性排序表示的可解释嵌入空间，但论文质疑当前模型是否真正利用双曲几何。其出发点是检验曲率在模型中的实际作用，尤其是指数映射是否带来了实质性的非欧几里得结构。

Result: 在MERU、HyCoCLIP、ARGENT和PHyCLIP上，指数映射可被恒等函数良好近似，映射前后成对距离和角度几乎不变，推理时移除映射评估分数不变。欧式VLM在CC3M上从零训练后，约最后90%训练步的统计与双曲版本几乎不可区分，并匹配评估分数、恢复相同径向分布。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于对双曲VLMs进行系统性实证诊断，指出当前实践中双曲几何可能未被真正利用，欧式空间已足以支撑现有层级视觉语言学习。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是围绕指数映射做消融与反事实欧式基线验证，从而挑战“双曲带来增益”的默认假设，并推动更有效利用双曲几何的新范式。

Abstract: Hyperbolic vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as a rapidly growing area of research, reporting strong zero-shot performance alongside an interpretable embedding space in which radius orders representations by generality. But how hyperbolic are hyperbolic VLMs in practice? We investigate this at the exponential map, the single operation through which curvature enters an otherwise Euclidean network. We show that across MERU, HyCoCLIP, ARGENT, and PHyCLIP, the exponential map is well approximated by the identity function, i.e., pairwise distances and angles in the pre-map Euclidean space barely change under projection, and removing the map at inference leaves evaluation scores unchanged. Motivated by this observation, we train Euclidean VLMs on CC3M from scratch under the same hierarchy losses. Over roughly the final 90% of the training steps, the training statistics are nearly indistinguishable from those of their hyperbolic counterparts, and the resulting models match the evaluation scores and recover the same radial distributions. Our findings establish Euclidean space as a sufficient substrate for hierarchical vision-language learning as it is currently practiced, and motivate new paradigms in which hyperbolic geometry can deliver on its theoretical promise. Code is available at https://github.com/fbyrman/hyperbolic-vlms.

Abstract: This paper tackles the task of hand-object contact transition detection in egocentric video, which aims to localize the two temporal boundaries of a contact episode: the moment a hand first establishes contact with an object (touch) and the moment contact is released (release). We propose a unified framework that jointly models the interacting hand and object to accurately localize both transition types. The proposed framework augments hand-centric spatiotemporal representations with object-aware features learned directly from the video and introduces a transition-aware event spotting formulation tailored to contact transitions, without relying on external object or hand-object interaction detectors during inference. To support this task, we present ContactTransition, a dataset comprising 4,021 egocentric videos with 8,498 touch and 8,077 release annotations, together with object-of-interest supervision. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong two-stage and end-to-end event-spotting baselines on both transition types. Details

Motivation: SDR-to-HDR重建是病态问题，需恢复高光、暗部和量化伪影；现有潜在扩散模型虽有生成先验，但HDR信号通过预训练VAE时会被不当编码，造成暗部色度信息丢失和潜在表示塌缩。

Abstract: SDR-to-HDR reconstruction is an ill-posed problem in which clipped highlights, crushed shadows, and quantization artifacts must be recovered while preserving the original content. Latent diffusion models provide powerful generative priors for restoring missing detail, but their effectiveness is constrained by how HDR signals pass through the pretrained VAE. We find that common log-like encodings suppress low-luminance chromatic differences before generation, producing a dark-side collapse in the frozen-VAE latent. We introduce EquivFlow, a compact, invertible exposure-equivariant encoding that maps exposure changes to additive shifts while retaining a scale-independent chromatic component, without modifying the VAE. Abstract: Curvilinear structures such as retinal vessels and surface cracks need both pixel-level accuracy and structural continuity. Pixel-wise supervision does not model the connections between pixels, and topology-based losses still produce ordinary masks. Connectivity-based methods predict these connections directly, but they use binary connectivity targets over a fixed local range. Such labels give the same value to thin segments and to thick regions, and they cannot describe connections at larger offsets. To address these limitations, we propose Topology-preserving Multi-offset Affinity Learning (TMAL). TMAL uses soft affinities that emphasize thin regions near the boundary, and it predicts directional affinities at several offsets to reach further. It then combines these affinities with weights that come from the decoder features, so local continuity and wider context are used together. An auxiliary segmentation branch adds region-level supervision and keeps the overlap accuracy high. On the retinal vessel dataset DRIVE and the crack inspection dataset DeepCrack, TMAL reaches the highest Dice, IoU, and clDice scores, and it greatly reduces the connected-component error $Δβ_0$. Code is available at https://github.com/suho-han/TMAL.

Existing traffic-video pipelines extract detections, trajectories, and risk scores but process footage uniformly, without adapting to an analyst’s query. Vision-language methods improve scene understanding but often lack traffic-specific verification of participant-level conflicts. We introduce AgenticConflictLens, an agentic, query-conditioned framework for efficient, auditable conflict extraction from long fixed-camera traffic videos. Given a natural-language safety query, a Traffic-Aware Controller combines structured traffic memory with specialized conflict-analysis tools to choose where to search, which road users to examine, and which evidence to verify or reject. A coarse-to-fine search retrieves candidate intervals and applies detailed verification selectively. The framework produces conflict records with refined time intervals, participant pairs, semantic regions, trajectories, motion evidence, uncertainty, rejection reasons, and supporting clips. On 96 annotated hours from Bellevue and NYC DOT, AgenticConflictLens achieves the highest conflict-existence F1 and temporal IoU on both datasets: 0.84 F1/0.77 tIoU on Bellevue and 0.80 F1/0.73 tIoU on NYC DOT, while inspecting only 8.7% and 11.9% of video, respectively. These results support scalable, evidence-grounded urban traffic-safety analysis.

Details

Motivation: 现有 T2V 物理常识评测依赖标注者凭直觉给单个总分，评分主观且无法指出视频具体违反了哪条物理原理，因此需要更可解释、可诊断的评估方法。

Result: 在十个 T2V 模型上的实验表明物理常识仍远未解决；PhyRubric-Judge 与人类判断的相关性优于现有开源 judge，并与前沿闭源模型 Gemini 4 Argon 相当。

Abstract: As text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly positioned as world simulators, it is essential to evaluate whether their videos conform to physical commonsense. However, existing benchmarks rely on annotators who rate each video with a single score based on intuition, yielding ratings that are subjective and do not tell which physical principle is violated. We introduce PhyRubric, a rubric-grounded diagnostic framework for evaluating physical commonsense in T2V generation. For each caption, PhyRubric constructs a caption-specific rubric before any video is seen, decomposing the caption into semantic and physical items, and derives scores from item-level verdicts grounded in timestamped evidence. We instantiate this framework as PhyRubric-Bench, which spans 34 categories across five physical domains, and scale it up to PhyRubric-Train with 3,788 videos, on which we fine-tune the open-source PhyRubric-Judge. Empirical results across ten T2V models show that physical commonsense remains far from solved, and that PhyRubric-Judge correlates more closely with human judgments than existing open-source judges while matching the frontier proprietary Gemini 4 Argon. Further analysis provides actionable insights for T2V models.

TL;DR: 论文研究生成式视频压缩中压缩潜变量进入扩散过程的去噪起始时间步选择问题，将其形式化为几何优化问题，并提出首个时间步感知的生成视频压缩框架 ADE-GVC。该方法用轻量预测器从压缩潜变量估计自适应去噪起点，并通过时间适配器将压缩感知条件注入预训练视频扩散模型，以兼顾重建保真度和感知真实感。

Details

Motivation: 扩散式生成视频压缩在极低码率下性能优越，但其效果关键取决于压缩潜变量何时引入扩散去噪过程，这直接影响重建保真度与感知真实感之间的权衡。论文旨在解决去噪起始时间步选择这一关键但此前未被充分建模的问题。

Result: 实验表明 ADE-GVC 在感知指标上达到 SOTA，并显著提升现有生成视频压缩方法的重建真实感。相较最新开源基线 GLC-Video，平均节省 40.99% 码率（DISTS）和 95.24% 码率（PSNR）。

Insight: 创新点在于将去噪起始时间步选择显式建模为几何优化问题，并提出时间步感知的自适应扩散入口，结合轻量预测器与时间适配器注入压缩感知条件。论文还发现预测时间步与原始/压缩潜变量失真之间存在强定量关系，为优化建模提供经验支持。

Abstract: Generative video compression offers a promising solution for compressing and reconstructing videos under extremely low bitrate constraints, among which the diffusion-based solutions still achieve the best performance by providing the powerful generative latent prior. However, their effectiveness critically depends on when the compressed latent is introduced into the diffusion process, which directly governs the trade-off between reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism. To address this limitation, we identify denoising start-timestep selection as a key factor governing this trade-off and formulate it as a geometric optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we propose a novel adaptive diffusion entry method for generative video compression (ADE-GVC), the first timestep-aware generative video compression framework, featuring a lightweight predictor that estimates an adaptive denoising start point from the compressed latent. A temporal adapter further extracts compression-aware conditions and injects them into a pretrained video diffusion model, enabling the reconstruction of videos with both high fidelity and realism. More importantly, we observe a strong quantitative relationship between the predicted timesteps and the distortion between the original and compressed latents, providing empirical support for our optimization formulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADE-GVC achieves the state-of-the-art performance on perceptual metrics and substantially improves reconstruction realism over existing generative video compression methods. Notably, our ADE-GVC achieves an average bitrate savings of 40.99% (on DISTS) and 95.24% (on PSNR) compared to the latest open-source baseline GLC-Video.

Motivation: 人体行为与 3D 场景的交互依赖复杂，准确预测人的意图是人体运动预测的主要挑战。现有利用 gaze 推断意图的方法存在歧义、漂移和依赖眼动硬件的问题；认知科学表明第一人称视角天然编码潜在交互，头部运动是运动规划的重要前兆。

Abstract: The complex interactive dependencies between human behavior and 3D scenes make accurately predicting human intent a major challenge for human motion prediction (HMP). While recent studies use human gaze to infer intent, gaze signals suffer from ambiguity, drift, and a reliance on eye-tracking hardware. Research in cognitive science indicates that egocentric views inherently encode potential interactions, while head motion serves as a crucial precursor in motion planning. Motivated by this, we propose EgoHMP, a novel framework that uses egocentric views and head motions as robust carriers of interaction intent. By translating this intent into an envisioning of future interactions, EgoHMP achieves precise HMP in 3D scenes. EgoHMP first employs a vision-motion alignment encoder to align the visual and motion feature spaces. Based on these aligned features, an egocentric intent predictor with a modality-aware modulation mechanism adaptively suppresses invalid interaction cues to predict an accurate interaction probability map. Furthermore, to enhance the stability and realism of the generated motions, we propose a contact-aware motion decoder. Specifically, inspired by the human cognitive process of planning trajectories prior to action execution, we first predict a global trajectory to serve as a self-prompt, effectively mitigating the optimization conflict in diffusion models. Subsequently, we introduce a multi-stage denoiser, which relieves motion artifacts by incorporating human contact modeling through a multi-stage optimization strategy. Finally, a GCN-based motion decoder is employed to synthesize physically plausible and semantically consistent motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EgoHMP achieves state-of-the-art performance.

Details

Motivation: 现有生成式视频修复模型能填充已知区域，但实际掩码来自检测器或分割器，存在漏检和误覆盖两类错误；将掩码当孔洞的擦除器会继承所有这些定位错误。论文旨在提升视频覆盖物去除对不完美定位的鲁棒性。

Result: 摘要未报告定量 benchmark 结果或 SOTA 对比，主要给出定性结果，并在项目页展示输入、提示和输出并排的视频结果。
