Abstract: Video overlay removal recovers the scene beneath burned-in subtitles, karaoke lyrics, titles, credits, logos and watermarks. Generative video models now fill a known region convincingly, but in practice the region comes from a detector or a segmenter, and it is wrong in both directions: it misses strokes, halos, whole frames and whole objects, and it covers background between characters, around boxes and on genuine scene text. Erasers that treat the mask as a hole, regenerating inside and copying outside, inherit every one of these errors. We present HintErase, which treats localization as a hint, not a hole. The model sees the full, unmasked video together with a hint channel; during training the hint is corrupted the way real localizers fail, while supervision follows the true overlay. The result is a single model that removes overlay the hint missed, keeps background the hint wrongly covered, and is trained to keep genuine scene text even when the hint lands on it. For full-length releases, a streaming pipeline handles interlaced sources, shot cuts, missed and jittering hints, paste-back and audio, with memory independent of the length of the video. This report describes the approach, the pipeline that turns it into full-length deliveries, and qualitative results; video results, with input, hint and output side by side, are on the project page.

TL;DR: 论文研究模块化神经网络中模块间通信应如何组织，提出 SyncNet：将模块内容与动态相位状态分离，用 Kuramoto 动力学演化相位，并由相位对齐决定共享通道中的通信。方法在 Sort-of-CLEVR 的关系推理和 SQOOP 的系统泛化上评估，其中 SQOOP 在最低 pair variety 设置下表现最好，但消融表明性能提升主要来自模块化分解而非同步机制本身。

Details

Motivation: 模块化网络旨在让不同模块学习可复用功能并支持组合泛化，但仅靠模块结构不足以保证功能专门化， unrestricted communication 会破坏专门化，因此需要研究模块间通信的组织方式，并借鉴感知绑定问题中的同步机制。

Result: 在 SQOOP 三个最低 pair variety 设置上取得对比模型中的最佳 held-out 准确率，最难设置下领先 5.1 个百分点；在 Sort-of-CLEVR 上有竞争力但低于最强 attention 基线。测试时干预显示固定相位与无寻址一样差，置换模块-区域对应也几乎同样有害；从头训练的内容寻址变体可匹配 SyncNet，说明收益可能来自模块化分解而非同步。

Insight: 创新点是用动态相位对齐控制模块通信，将 Kuramoto 同步机制引入模块化网络以协调分布式表示。客观看，论文的重要贡献还包括对同步必要性的批判性消融：相位演化与模块-区域对应虽影响性能，但内容寻址变体可匹配，提示真正收益主要来自模块化结构而非同步本身。

Abstract: One motivation for modular neural networks is to divide computation across components that learn distinct, reusable functions, which could then be recombined to support compositional generalisation. In practice, modular structure alone does not guarantee that such roles develop, and unrestricted communication between modules can undermine any specialisation that does emerge. This raises the question of how modular communication should be organised. The problem can be viewed as an analogue of the perceptual binding problem, where synchrony has been proposed as a mechanism for coordinating distributed representations. We introduce SyncNet, a modular architecture that separates module content from a dynamical phase state: learnt Kuramoto dynamics evolve the phases, while phase alignment determines communication through a shared channel. We evaluate SyncNet on Sort-of-CLEVR for relational reasoning and on SQOOP for systematic generalisation, where lower pair variety demands more recombination at test time. SyncNet gives the best held-out accuracy of the models compared at the three lowest pair varieties, by 5.1 percentage points at the hardest, and is competitive on Sort-of-CLEVR though below the strongest attention baselines. Test-time overrides show that performance depends on phases that evolve: fixed phases cost as much as no addressing at all, and permuting which module holds which region is nearly as damaging. A content-addressed variant trained from scratch nonetheless matches it, so the benefit appears to come from the modular decomposition rather than synchrony itself.

Abstract: Remote sensing disaster assessment is moving from simple damage classification toward comprehensive report-level analysis. DisasterAgent first invokes a specialized vision expert toolkit in the perception phase to build a Spatiotemporal Perception Graph (STPG), serving as a structured memory to store and organize disaster-induced changes across space and time. Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Predicate-State Video Reasoning Segmentation (PS-VRS), where a query defines a dynamic predicate $P(o,t)$ over objects and time rather than a fixed object identity. Result: 实验显示 SemPair 的下游迁移性能与精确配对 ExaPair 相当；MixPair 揭示下游迁移性能与实例配对比例之间呈非单调关系。

Abstract: Multimodal remote sensing pretraining commonly relies on exact instance-level correspondence across modalities, implicitly treating strictly paired observations as a prerequisite for learning transferable representations. In this work, we revisit this assumption by relaxing cross-modal correspondence from the instance level to the semantic level. We introduce Semantic Pairing (SemPair), a pairing-relaxed pretraining strategy that replaces exact instance pairs with class-consistent cross-instance pairs. To characterize the effect of correspondence relaxation, we formulate a latent-factor model that separates cross-modal dependence into shared semantic structure and instance-specific correlation, showing that SemPair preserves the former while marginalizing instance-specific cross-modal correlations in expectation. We further introduce Mixed Pairing (MixPair) as a controlled interpolation between semantic and exact pairing to study correspondence granularity along a continuous spectrum. Extensive experiments consistently show that SemPair achieves transfer performance comparable to exact pairing (ExaPair), while MixPair reveals a non-monotonic relationship between downstream transfer and the instance-pairing ratio. These results demonstrate that exact instance correspondence is not a prerequisite for effective multimodal remote sensing pretraining and establish cross-modal correspondence granularity as an important design dimension for transferable multimodal representation learning. More broadly, this finding suggests a practical route toward scaling multimodal pretraining with reduced dependence on costly instance-aligned data.

[58] CASCADE: Constraint-Aware SCAffolded DEcomposition for Logical Anomaly Detection cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

TL;DR: 论文研究逻辑异常检测（LAD），即仅给定少量正常参考图像，判断查询图像是否满足类别特定约束（如目标计数、配对关系和测量范围）。现有基于LVLM的checklist验证将决策分解为独立文本检查，但每个检查仍读取同一张原始图像，导致文本-图像不匹配的约束特定感知鸿沟，并引发绑定约束的实例混淆和校准约束的尺度漂移。作者提出免训练框架CASCADE，用约束特定视觉支架扩展checklist分解，并进一步提出对象级自适应支架的CASCADE++。

Details

Motivation: 现有LVLM逻辑异常检测方法主要做文本层面的checklist分解，却未针对不同约束调整视觉输入，使模型需从同一原始图像中提取不同形式的视觉证据。论文旨在诊断该失败源于推理不足还是感知不足，并解决约束特定感知鸿沟带来的实例混淆与尺度漂移问题。

Result: 在MVTec-LOCO和VID-AD上，CASCADE++仅用3张参考图像即比全样本SOTA高3.9个AUROC点，并在VID-AD上比最强基线提升11.4个点；方法免训练且可迁移到多个LVLM骨干。

Insight: 创新点在于将文本checklist分解扩展到约束特定的视觉支架，使每个约束获得适配的视觉输入，并由约束结构和参考分布推导对象级支架配置。客观来看，论文的关键洞察是逻辑异常检测的瓶颈更多在约束特定感知而非文本推理，通过视觉输入自适应而非仅改进推理提示来提升验证准确性。

Abstract: Logical Anomaly Detection (LAD) requires verifying whether all category-specific constraints, such as object counts, pairing relations, and measurement ranges, are satisfied by a query image given only a few normal references. Recent LAD methods based on Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) formulate this as checklist verification, decomposing the decision into independent textual checks. This decomposition, however, is text-only. Every item still reads the same unmodified query image, so the LVLM must extract different forms of visual evidence from one raw image. We identify this text-image mismatch as a constraint-specific perception gap, the source of two failure modes: instance conflation for binding constraints and scale drift for calibration constraints. To diagnose whether these failures stem from insufficient reasoning or insufficient perception, we compare textual reasoning guides and visual perception guides in a training-free setting. Our analysis shows that reasoning over the same image provides little benefit, whereas adapting the visual input to each constraint substantially improves verification accuracy. Based on this finding, we propose CASCADE, a training-free framework that extends checklist decomposition using constraint-specific visual scaffolds. We further introduce CASCADE++, which adapts these scaffolds at the object level by deriving their configurations from the constraint structure and reference distribution. Across MVTec-LOCO and VID-AD, CASCADE++ surpasses the full-shot state of the art by 3.9 AUROC points with only three references, and improves over the strongest VID-AD baseline by 11.4, transferring across multiple LVLM backbones without any training.

Details

Motivation: 传统遥感场景分类依赖图像视觉表征，论文希望探究仅使用文本语义表征能否识别遥感场景，并建立一个统一、可复现的纯文本分类基准，以研究模态缩减场景理解的可行性。

Result: 在三个知名遥感场景分类数据集上的实验表明，语义代理能够保留大量判别信息，同时也揭示了纯文本场景理解的固有局限；摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA结论。

Insight: 主要创新在于提出 text-only RSSC 新问题、通过 VLM 生成语义代理并解耦为文本分类流程，以及系统分析提示策略、代理生成器、跨生成器迁移性和效率，为高效、模态缩减的遥感场景理解提供基准与分析基础。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene classification (RSSC) is traditionally formulated as a vision-centric problem, where inference relies on rich visual representations extracted from images. In this work, we revisit this assumption by introducing text-only RSSC, a new problem setting that investigates whether remote sensing scenes can be recognized using only textual semantic representations. To enable this study, we propose SeProRS, which reformulates RSSC as a text-only classification task through semantic proxy generation. Specifically, a vision-language model (VLM) is first employed to convert remote sensing images into textual descriptions, termed semantic proxies, which are then encoded by a text embedding model for scene classification. This decoupled pipeline provides a unified and reproducible benchmark for evaluating text-only scene understanding. Furthermore, we investigate representative prompting strategies and proxy generators, and analyze their impact on classification performance, text embedding behavior, cross-generator transferability, and efficiency. Extensive experiments on three well-known RSSC datasets show that semantic proxies can preserve substantial discriminative information, while also revealing inherent limitations of purely text-based scene understanding. This paper does not aim to replace image-based RSSC, but instead establishes a new research direction by quantifying the feasibility and boundaries of semantic proxy-based scene recognition. The proposed benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for future research on efficient and modality-reduced remote sensing scene understanding. The source code and datasets will be accessible at https://github.com/sstary/SSRS.

Motivation: 现有预训练图像生成器用于语言引导UAV分割时存在“Grounding-Rendering Gap”：渲染出的掩码可能合理，但在小而重复的目标中对应错误实例。论文旨在解决掩码渲染与语言表达级目标选择之间的不匹配。

Result: 在RIS-LAD上达到52.78 mIoU，较此前最佳提升7.16点，取得新的SOTA；在UAVid-RIS和VDD-RIS上整体保持竞争力，并在车辆、行人等紧凑且应用相关类别上有明显类别级增益。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that pretrained image generators can support diverse visual tasks. Yet directly applying them to language-guided unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) segmentation exposes a key limitation: a plausible mask may correspond to the wrong instance among small and repeated objects. We call this mismatch between mask rendering and expression-level target selection the Grounding-Rendering Gap. We present DroneSegEdit, which combines RGB proposal rendering with language-grounded, proposal-constrained verification to select supported foreground while retaining rendered geometry. DroneSegEdit adapts the open-weight LongCat-Image-Edit model using task-specific LoRA and fixed color-to-label decoding. For referring segmentation, a Language-Grounded Spatial Verifier (LGSV) reads hierarchical states from the frozen multimodal large language model backbone to derive expression-conditioned spatial evidence, which is mapped back to the rendered proposal without introducing foreground outside it. On RIS-LAD, DroneSegEdit reaches a new state of the art at 52.78 mIoU, 7.16 points above the previous best. It remains competitive overall on UAVid-RIS and VDD-RIS, with its clearest class-wise gains on compact, application-relevant categories, notably vehicles and humans. A separate verifier-free experiment extends the RGB interface to full-scene parsing: a task-specific adapter jointly renders all classes from one task-level instruction without enumerating class names or palette colors.

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene graphs (3DSGs) can provide robots with persistent metric-semantic representations for spatial reasoning, but the existing methods have primarily been developed for indoor and terrestrial environments. In this paper we investigate how a representative outdoor open-vocabulary method transfers to maritime environments and which adaptations are needed. Our experiments show that zero-shot representative outdoor 3DSG descriptions agree with our labeled ground truth for a majority of geometry-supported objects on our data, but the resulting map exhibits considerable physical-entity fragmentation, and its terrestrial higher-level assumptions don’t capture maritime spatial organization needs. We therefore keep the underlying mapping method and post-process its accumulated evidence. We show a metric-semantic consolidation method followed by geometry-grounded multi-view vision-language model (VLM) reasoning that reconciles remaining fragments into physical entities and groups entities through observations into local places. In parallel, we construct maritime macro regions using map priors and onboard water and structure observations, and associate them with the local places. The resulting representation reduces physical-entity fragmentation beyond both the baseline and our geometry-semantic consolidation component alone, supports correspondence of fixed local places across repeated maritime traversals, and improves contextual retrieval on our reviewed queries over tested baselines. These results show how an outdoor open-vocabulary map can be adapted into persistent maritime scene memory with our methods for object/place identity and spatial abstractions.

Motivation: 固定帧预算下，采样帧是否足以支撑回答决定了模型应直接作答还是进行推理；现有方法难以根据证据充分性自适应选择响应模式，可能导致证据充分时过度推理或证据不足时盲目直答。

Result: 在多个视频理解 benchmark 上，EVAThink 在 32 帧和 64 帧预算下均持续优于基础模型，并在平均性能上超过强基线；训练仅使用 6.7K curated 样本。

Abstract: Under a fixed frame budget, video understanding with vision-language models (VLMs) depends on whether the sampled frames provide sufficient evidence for the question. Sufficient frame evidence supports direct answering, whereas less informative observations may benefit from reasoning that connects the available cues. We introduce EVAThink, which enhances video understanding through evidence sufficiency-guided training for adaptive thinking. First, Recoverable Evidence Gap Mining constructs evidence-dense and evidence-sparse video views that share the same question and answer under an identical frame budget. It retains pairs where sparse views yield lower direct-answer accuracy than dense views but benefit from explicit reasoning, providing supervision for response-mode selection. Second, Evidence-Guided Two-Stage Reinforcement Learning uses stage-specific rewards to guide training with evidence-based mode preferences and then refine response efficiency, while first-token advantage regularization mitigates mode collapse. Trained on only 6.7K curated samples, EVAThink consistently improves upon the base model across video understanding benchmarks under both 32- and 64-frame budgets, while outperforming strong baselines on average. The code, model, and datasets are available at \href{https://github.com/ChangtiWu/EVAThink}{EVAThink}.

Abstract: Natural fractures (NFs) and drilling-induced tensile fractures (DITFs) in acoustic image logs provide insights into rock mass structure and in situ stress, but manual characterization is time-consuming. Deep learning can automate this task, although expert-labeled data are scarce. We investigate whether adapting a pretrained vision foundation model (VFM) outperforms task-specific models trained from scratch with limited annotations. Using 80 annotated acoustic image samples, we fine-tuned DINOv3, pretrained on satellite imagery, and evaluated it on four boreholes excluded from fine-tuning. The adapted model estimated NF and DITF parameters comparably to human interpretation. Abstract: Multi-modal brain tumor segmentation is essential for clinical diagnosis, radiotherapy planning, and longitudinal monitoring. While recent studies have achieved remarkable overall segmentation performance, they rely on overlap-based objectives that inherently favor large regions, causing sub-3 mm micro-lesions to be systematically suppressed during training. Moreover, existing pixel-level segmentation frameworks lack explicit instance-level modeling, leading to unreliable lesion counting that directly impacts clinical staging and therapeutic decisions. To address these challenges, we propose MicroBT, a unified multi-modal framework for micro brain tumor segmentation and counting. Our core insight is that a fundamental geometric property is inherently scale-invariant: regardless of size, each lesion possesses a well-defined centroid. Leveraging this, we first devise a centroid-anchored synergistic paradigm that utilizes predicted centroids as shared anchors to simultaneously condition dense segmentation and instance-level localization. To counteract the tumor size imbalance, we further devise a Volume-Calibrated Loss (VC-Loss) that adaptively amplifies the gradient contribution of micro-lesions, alongside a Size-Aware Curriculum Optimization (SACO) that stabilizes multi-task training by establishing global representations before progressively unlocking fine-grained localization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MicroBT outperforms state-of-the-art methods, particularly on metastases, where micro-lesion ($\leq 3$ mm) localization precision improves by approximately 7% and counting estimation errors decrease by approximately 26%. The code is available at https://github.com/leonlou921/MicroBT.

Result: 简单色度轨迹在说话人脸伪造上AUC达0.998，高于专用rPPG的0.87；在Celeb-DF++上训练的基于人脸色度检测器将超过99%的真实FF++视频判为伪造；冻结CLIP骨干网络可匹配或超过CLIP与rPPG及频率特征的融合；复用测试视频和身份的自定义协议可使较弱检测器AUC虚高最多0.30。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于通过打乱帧序、去除或添加高频、从背景或全帧提取、注入合成脉搏、自重现控制实验和独立复现等手段，证明rPPG式检测器主要编码高频生成伪影与视频来源而非真实生理信号，并指出Celeb-DF系列评测协议存在数据泄漏，对深伪检测的可解释性和基准可靠性提出重要警示。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) has been proposed as a physiological cue for deepfake detection, based on the premise that synthesized faces lack a genuine cardiac signal. We examine what rPPG-style detectors learn on Celeb-DF++ (22 forgery methods) and FaceForensics++ (FF++), using one identically sampled window per real and fake video. On talking-face forgeries, simple chrominance traces reach an AUC of 0.998, exceeding a dedicated rPPG extractor (0.87). Controlled experiments indicate that this signal is not physiological in origin. On fully synthesized forgeries, it is equally discriminative when extracted from background regions or from full frames without face detection, and it is concentrated above the 0.7 to 4 Hz heart-rate band. It is largely removed by shuffling frame order but persists after normalizing resolution, frame rate and encoding, and an independent reimplementation reproduces these findings. Removing high-frequency content makes most synthesized videos appear real, adding it makes real videos appear fake, and injecting a synthetic pulse has little effect. In controlled self-reenactment experiments with two generators, animating a face from a single frame introduces the same high-frequency signal, whereas reconstructing each frame from its own source does not. Face-based chrominance detectors trained on Celeb-DF++ classify over 99% of real FF++ videos as fake, indicating that they largely encode video source rather than manipulation, and a CLIP-based detector exhibits the same behavior. A frozen CLIP backbone matches or exceeds fusions of CLIP with rPPG and frequency features. Finally, we show that the official Celeb-DF and Celeb-DF++ splits share identities and source-video content between training and test, and that custom protocols reusing test videos and identities in training inflate weaker detectors by up to 0.30 AUC.

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities but raise significant privacy concerns due to their abilities to infer sensitive personal information from images with high precision. While current LVLMs are relatively well aligned to protect universal privacy, e.g., credit card data, we argue that privacy is inherently personalized and context-dependent. This work pivots towards a novel task: can LVLMs achieve Inference-Time Personalized Privacy Protection (ITP$^3$), allowing users to dynamically specify privacy boundaries through language specifications? To this end, we present SPY-Bench, the first systematic assessment of ITP$^3$ ability, which comprises (1) 32,700 unique samples with image-question pairs and personalized privacy instructions across 67 categories and 24 real-world scenarios, and (2) novel metrics grounded in user specifications and context awareness. Benchmarking the ITP$^3$ ability of 21 SOTA LVLMs, we reveal that: (i) most models, even the top-performing o4-mini, perform poorly, with only $\sim$24% compliance accuracy; (ii) they show quite limited contextual privacy understanding capability. Therefore, we implemented initial ITP$^3$ alignment methods, including a novel Noise Contrastive Alignment variant which achieves 96.88% accuracy while maintaining reasonable general performance. These results mark an initial step towards the ethical deployment of more controllable LVLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/achernarwang/specify-privacy-yourself

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation can draft clinically plausible reports, yet the pathway from visual evidence to reported findings remains opaque, leaving the diagnostic basis of these reports unexplained and the generated reports difficult to review or revise. To support this process, we constructed 159,416 multimodal chain-of-thought (MCoT) trajectories from MIMIC-CXR, wherein visual evidence from inspected anatomical regions informs subsequent clinical interpretation. Motivation: 实时机器人感知需要在有限资源下获得准确LiDAR语义。BEV聚合虽高效，但同一BEV列中不同标签表面可能被混合，导致语义边界和垂直结构信息受损。

Abstract: Real-time robotic perception requires accurate LiDAR semantics under limited resources. Bird’s-eye-view (BEV) aggregation is efficient but can mix differently labeled surfaces in columns. We present HiPFuse, a point-BEV fusion framework that exchanges point and height-partitioned BEV features across horizontal scales, combining point-wise detail with 2D spatial context from one LiDAR scan. Fixed height assignments preserve separate vertical aggregation groups, with auxiliary supervision of initial BEV features using targets from the same 3D cells. [70] Few-Step Streaming Video Generation via Condition Shifting cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Deyuan Liu, Jungang Li, Kaiwen Zheng, Kai Jiang, Jintao Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 APEX，将条件偏移（condition shifting）用于流式视频生成中的少步采样：用原始文本条件驱动生成，用偏移条件在同一生成器参数下估计生成分布的伪速度场，从而替代 DMD 中额外的 fake-score 网络。方法保留 S2-Avatar 的冻结参考教师、Self-Replay Forcing 和感知正则，并在 180 秒视频、人类研究和 StreamAV-Bench 上验证了指令遵循、视觉美学和训练效率。

Details

Motivation: 少步采样可降低流式视频生成成本，但 DMD 需要独立 fake-score 网络，增加训练显存和计算，并且该网络必须跟踪不断演化的生成器。论文希望去掉这一独立网络，同时保持少步流式视频生成质量。

Result: 在 StreamAV-Bench 等评测中，APEX 在 Progressive track 的视频指令遵循得分 3.856 高于 S2-Avatar 的 3.831，视觉美学 0.641898 高于 0.632606；人类评估中指令遵循净偏好 +30.0%，一致性和视频质量各 +4.3%，运动质量 S2-Avatar 更优，音视频同步基本持平。匹配训练设置下，相比 DMD 每 GPU 峰值分配显存降低 4.4%，平均迭代时间缩短 9.2%。

Insight: 核心创新是用条件偏移在同一生成器内估计生成分布的伪速度场：原始条件选择生成，偏移条件选择伪估计，从而移除独立 fake-score 网络并避免其跟踪生成器。客观看，该设计将 DMD 式蒸馏的伪评分估计内化到生成器中，在保留参考教师和正则的同时提升显存与迭代效率，是少步流式视频生成中较实用的蒸馏改进。

Abstract: Few-step sampling reduces the cost of streaming video generation. Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) supervises few-step generators with a separate fake-score network, which adds training memory and computation and must track the evolving generator. We extend APEX to streaming video by using condition shifting to estimate the velocity field of the generated distribution within the generator. The original text condition selects generation, while a shifted condition selects fake estimation using the same parameters. This removes the independent fake network while retaining S2-Avatar’s frozen reference teacher, Self-Replay Forcing, and perceptual regularization. We evaluate APEX on 180-second videos using three backbone evaluations per block and a one-step spatial Refiner, with a 70-case human study and a 320-case StreamAV-Bench comparison. Compared with S2-Avatar, APEX achieves higher video instruction fulfillment on the Progressive track (3.856 versus 3.831) and higher visual aesthetics across both tracks (0.641898 versus 0.632606). Human raters favor APEX most strongly on instruction following, with +30.0% net preference, followed by consistency and video quality at +4.3% each. Motion quality favors S2-Avatar, audiovisual synchronization is predominantly tied, and usability is unchanged. Separate measurements under matched training settings show 4.4% lower peak allocated GPU memory per GPU and 9.2% shorter mean iteration time than DMD.

Abstract: A vision-language model may take left-right direction from the orientation of image content within patches, its vision transformer’s (ViT) position code, or token positions and order in its language model (LLM). Ablations show the ViT code is necessary; we test sufficiency by mirroring only a position code. In Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B and InternVL3-2B, mirroring ViT width positions reproduces the flipped-image answer on 84.3-93.2% of CV-Bench left-right questions (Qwen2.5-VL-3B: 87.5%, partly a left fallback). On What’sUp, flipping content and token order, not the position code, keeps more on/under than left-right answers at the flipped-image answer. Post hoc analyses qualify these results. On CV-Bench items with located objects, random permutations flip 18.9-31.0 points more answers when reversing the objects’ order than when keeping it. On What’sUp, in Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL, the share of the left-right changes made by random permutations that land on the mirror relation is mostly below chance. Counted as returns to the original answer, the What’sUp contrast of support with left-right relations is clear only in InternVL3-2B. Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception requires Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to locate and interpret subtle evidence within complex scenes. Constructing supervision for this ability is challenging: questions must have correct, unambiguous answers that depend on actual fine-grained visual details rather than coarse cues or language priors. Verifying these properties makes manual annotation labor-intensive and automatic generation difficult for models that themselves struggle with fine-grained perception. Yet the same models can often recognize such details once the region is zoomed in. We exploit this global-to-zoom perception gap and introduce ZoomGain, a data construction pipeline that constructs questions about the full image from visual information gained only by zooming in. It verifies each answer on the zoomed view with multiple models, ensures that each question remains unambiguous in the full image and cannot be answered from language priors, and specifically generates high-quality distractors for multiple-choice questions. With this pipeline and human-only screening (no manual QA editing), we build ZoomGainBench, a 1,000-question bilingual benchmark on which the best of 28 VLMs achieves only 61.08. Failure analysis reveals that models favor a particular type of distractor when answering multiple-choice questions incorrectly. Beyond evaluation, training on just 9K ZoomGain samples substantially improves the visual perception capabilities of our 4B/9B models, enabling them to outperform several hundred-billion-parameter models across various perception benchmarks. The trained 9B model achieves SoTA performance on several benchmarks, such as 90.25 on HR-Bench 4K. These results establish global-to-zoom perception gaps as a practical basis for scaling fine-grained visual supervision for both evaluation and training. Our code is available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/ZoomGain.

Motivation: 多模态大模型生成的视频描述往往缺少区分相邻状态所需的关键属性。论文希望借助对下一步变化的预期，主动选择当前帧中需要观察的属性，从而更准确地读取物体状态及其转换。

Abstract: Understanding the progress of an activity requires tracking how the objects involved change over time. Object state readout provides this information by describing a target object’s states and transitions across video frames. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate detailed visual descriptions, these descriptions may leave the attributes needed to distinguish successive states insufficiently specified. In this paper, we propose look-ahead state readout (LASER), an anticipation-guided agent for object state readout that uses possible next-step changes to determine which attributes to inspect in the present. Given temporally ordered frames and a target object, LASER converts anticipated changes into targeted visual questions and inspects the current frame to establish attribute values. It then checks state readouts and forecasts against independent observations of subsequent frames, refining a state record used to generate the final description. Reusable textual observation skills guide this process and are refined by tracing discrepancies with reference captions to the responsible instructions. Candidate revisions are selected on separate videos based on improved caption quality without increased contradictions with reference facts. Model parameters remain frozen throughout adaptation and inference, and the revised skills remain fixed during evaluation. Experiment results and further analysis on OSCaR and PLM RCap show consistent improvements of LASER over strong baselines and existing approaches, which demonstrates the effectiveness of our approach.

Abstract: Remote sensing increasingly captures the Earth’s surface through complementary sensors such as SAR and optical, yet ground-truth annotations remain scarce, limiting supervised approaches. Details

Motivation: 流式多参考视频生成需要先根据指定人物和物体建立场景，再在变化指令下持续延续场景；现有按操作划分去噪与上下文写入的适配方式可能不适合这种时间角色差异，因此论文探索按场景建立/延续进行参数专门化。

Abstract: Streaming multi-reference video generation establishes a scene from specified people and objects, then continues it under changing instructions. We introduce TaoMate-R1, a streaming multi-reference generator that organizes adaptation parameters through temporal role specialization. It assigns separate low-rank adapters to scene establishment and continuation on a frozen backbone. Each adapter performs both denoising and context writing for its role; Self Gradient Forcing jointly trains the roles through the history they construct. This allocation contrasts with operation-based specialization, which separates denoising from context writing across all chunks. A fixed-replay gradient analysis characterizes the update directions admitted by the two sharing schemes. On 32 multi-reference cases, we compare the schemes at the same two-adapter budget. Details

Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models (VDMs) offer a powerful foundation for World Action Models (WAMs), which jointly predict robot actions and future observations. Building such models requires injecting low-dimensional robot signals (e.g. proprioception, actions, and values) into a latent space shaped by natural videos, and a key design choice is the interface through which these signals enter: existing schemes treat it as a formatting detail, spending a separate latent frame on each signal or bolting on dedicated encoders and decoders. We argue that signal injection is instead a distribution-alignment problem: how well a representation matches the prior of the pretrained VDMs during denoising. Using the relative reconstruction error of a frozen VDM denoiser as a probe, we find that conventional spatial encodings sit far off this prior, whereas frequency-domain packing yields latents that the denoiser corrects far less. Motivated by this diagnostic result, we propose MetaWAM, which folds all robot signals into a single unified meta-frame: each signal is assigned to a disjoint frequency-domain channel band and lifted to the spatial latent by an inverse DCT, yielding a decodable interface aligned with the pretrained VDM prior without any added module or architectural change. A fixed band layout together with slot-aware routing lets the same meta-frame support policy, world-model, and value prediction over a shared set of robot variables. On RoboCasa, MetaWAM reaches 68.9% success while collapsing the per-signal frames into one and cutting inference latency by 39%, and attains 85.0% success on three real-world Franka tasks. Code and models are available at https://github.com/showlab/MetaWAM

Result: 在12个跨领域计数任务上，现代多模态模型提供较强的免训练起点；微调检测器在更大标注预算和重复部署下更有优势；用模型预测优先安排人工标注比均匀采样更高效；小型试点集可估计模型排序质量。航拍叉角羚实地部署中，任务特定适配方法在审阅中位数仅3.2%图像后达到调查±5%容差。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于统一人机协同计数框架下系统比较模型、估计器与适配策略，并将模型预测用于主动标注优先级和试点评估，为新计数任务提供可操作的策略选择依据；客观看，其贡献更多是实践性基准、实证洞见和部署指南，而非单一全新算法。

Abstract: Many scientific and policy decisions require estimating the total number of target objects across a large image collection. Exhaustive human labeling is often infeasible, while raw model predictions can be biased, especially under domain shift. Recent human-AI estimation methods combine imperfect model predictions with limited human verification to obtain reliable collection-level estimates. However, existing work provides little guidance on how practitioners should choose among models, estimators, and adaptation strategies for a new deployment. We study these choices within a common human-in-the-loop counting framework across 12 tasks spanning biomedical microscopy, ground-level and aerial wildlife surveys, and overhead imagery of urban structures. Our results show that modern multimodal models provide a strong, training-free starting point for many tasks, while fine-tuned detectors become more advantageous at larger labeling budgets and across repeated deployments. Estimation efficiency improves further when model predictions are used to prioritize which units humans label, rather than sampling uniformly. We also find that each model’s ranking quality can be estimated with a small pilot set, yielding practical guidance for selecting strategies on new counting tasks. In a field deployment on aerial pronghorn surveys, a task-specific adaptation of these methods meets the survey’s $\pm 5%$ tolerance after reviewing a median of only $3.2%$ of collected images.

[83] OnlyAct: Extracting Action Representations from Foundation Models for World Models cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 OnlyAct，从预训练基础模型 V-JEPA 2 的表征中无标签地提取动作表示，用于视频世界模型。方法通过对比式适配拉近不同场景下相同运动的帧对、推远同场景不同运动的帧对，从而在丢弃场景信息的同时保留动作信息，避免从零学习动作空间和重建瓶颈。作者还在机器人操作、相机导航、游戏和驾驶等基准上提出统一评测协议，验证动作表达能力、场景不变性和动作条件视频生成质量。

Motivation: 视频世界模型训练受限于动作监督：动作标签稀缺、规格不统一且难以随无标注视频预训练语料扩展。常见做法是从零联合训练潜动作编码器和下一帧重建解码器，但存在泛化受限以及表达能力与场景分离之间的瓶颈权衡。

Abstract: Video world models promise general-purpose interactive simulators, but their training is limited by action supervision: action labels are scarce, fragmented across incompatible specifications, and do not scale with the unlabeled video pretraining corpus. A common workaround jointly trains an action encoder to compress consecutive frames into a latent action and a decoder to reconstruct the next frame from the previous frame and that latent, forcing the latent to capture only new information. This has two limitations: learning the action space from scratch can limit generalization to unseen domains, and the bottleneck trades expressiveness for separation, with tighter settings losing nuance and looser ones risking scene leakage under domain shifts. We argue for a different starting point: rather than learning actions from scratch, we start from a pretrained foundation model (V-JEPA 2), whose embeddings of a frame pair already carry substantial action information after large-scale, diverse video pretraining. The remaining challenge is to extract the action while discarding the scene. We propose a label-free contrastive adaptation framework that pulls together frame pairs sharing the same motion under a different scene and pushes apart pairs from the same scene with different motion, reshaping the geometry of the pretrained space without imposing a reconstruction bottleneck. We introduce a unified evaluation protocol along three axes (action expressiveness, scene invariance, and action-conditioned video generation quality) across robot manipulation, camera navigation, game, and driving benchmarks, most of which are unseen during adaptation. Compared with annotation-free latent-action methods, our representations are substantially more expressive while matching their scene invariance, and they yield world models that transfer demonstrated actions to new scenes more faithfully.

Abstract: Deepfake detectors are commonly evaluated on real/fake classification alone, but real-world applications require explanations of the visual evidence behind each prediction. Abstract: Understanding 4D scenes is a core challenge in visual intelligence, requiring models to jointly capture scene geometry, appearance, and dynamics from partial observations. However, existing approaches are subdivided into feedforward models for direct geometric prediction, and generative models for visual synthesis. We introduce UGG, a unified framework for 4D scene understanding that jointly models RGB video and geometric representations such as depth and camera pose. By assigning different noise levels across modalities and time, UGG formulates visual generation and geometric prediction as conditional completion within a single generative model. This flexible formulation enables the model to learn from diverse, heterogeneous datasets with different subsets of geometric and visual annotations. Furthermore, our generative formulation enables inference-time search over multiple plausible scene hypotheses, using geometric evidence extracted from observed RGB frames to improve geometric prediction without ground truth geometry. Details

Motivation: VQA计算开销大，需要模型无关的输入减少方法；简单限制视觉token数量会丢失信息，且单一减少策略难以适应不同场景，观察到裁剪突出局部细节而压缩保留全局上下文，二者互补。

Result: 在3个VLM和18个数据集上，以1024视觉token为基线，平均进一步减少端到端FLOPs 36.53%和视觉token 42.36%，平均相对任务分数仅下降0.77%。

Insight: 创新点在于发现裁剪与压缩的互补性并自适应路由，以及安全门机制利用原始和减少输入下的联合正确性分布指导回退。客观分析：结合互补策略动态选择思路新颖，安全门为减少信息损失提供保障，模型无关性使其适用于黑盒场景。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) imposes substantial computational overhead on vision-language models (VLMs), motivating model-agnostic input reduction methods that can be readily applied across diverse VLMs, including in black-box settings. Simply enforcing a visual-token cap reduces computation, but it can discard information needed to answer the question. A single reduction strategy also struggles to accommodate different scenarios. Our key observation is that cropping and compression are complementary: cropping highlights small targets and local details, whereas whole-image compression better preserves context for large targets or distributed evidence. Based on this observation, we propose C$^2$-Routing, which adaptively selects cropping or compression according to the input image and question. Since both strategies may still discard key information, we further design a safety gate that uses the predicted joint correctness distribution under original and reduced inputs to guide fallback and protect accuracy. To accommodate consumer GPU resource constraints, all training and evaluation experiments run on RTX 3090 GPUs. To measure gains beyond token capping, we use full-image baselines with the same cap of 1,024 visual tokens. We conduct extensive evaluation on three VLMs and 18 datasets spanning local details, global scenes, scene text, and documents. With the visual budget already capped, C$^2$-Routing further reduces end-to-end FLOPs by 36.53% and visual tokens by 42.36% on average, with a mean relative task-score decrease of only 0.77%.

Abstract: Computer vision offers a scalable, non-contact means of estimating livestock traits, but correlated images and video frames make reliable evaluation difficult. Image-random splitting can place views of the same animal in both training and test sets, while deployment further requires transfer to animals captured in future years. We introduce BoviShift, a corpus reconstructed from six consecutive annual cohorts of an operational cattle marketplace collected from 2021 to 2026. It contains 2{,}657 cattle, 10{,}610 still images, and 2{,}566 walk-around videos across nine recorded breed categories. We evaluate three splitting strategies through four configurations: image-random and pooled cattle-disjoint splits over the same development cohorts, followed by separate future-2025 and locked future-2026 evaluations. Using a fixed ResNet-50 multi-task baseline, we assess weight, age, height, breed, and coat color. Differences between image-random and cattle-disjoint splitting are task-dependent rather than a universal leakage penalty. Future-cohort behavior is also attribute- and cohort-specific: breed declines relative to pooled evaluation; mean weight and age performance fails to beat constant baselines in 2025; height remains above baseline; and color transfers well to 2025 but degrades in 2026. Multi-task training improves all five point estimates in a single-seed ablation, still-view aggregation helps selectively, and zero-shot video-frame aggregation exposes a still-to-video domain gap. Details

Motivation: 跨传感器遥感图像检索需要在无人工标签条件下匹配 SAR 与光学、全色与多光谱等异源图像；现有自监督方法存在重建传感器特定像素、仅对齐配准图像对或模型参数过大的问题。

Abstract: Cross-sensor remote sensing image retrieval aims to find scenes with the same content across heterogeneous sensors, such as SAR and optical or panchromatic (PAN) and multispectral (MS) imagery, without manual labels. Existing self-supervised methods either reconstruct sensor-specific pixel detail, align each image only with its co-registered twin, or rely on large transformers with 118-173M parameters (and up to 398M). We introduce RS-JEPA, a compact joint-embedding predictive framework built on a Vision Mamba encoder shared by both sensors. Since a state-space encoder reads an image as an ordered sequence of patches, we propose scan-path views, which treat two reading orders of the same image as two views with identical pixels and predict the hidden patches in one order from the visible patches in the other.To keep this prediction challenging, we propose Adversarial View Coupling (AVC), which learns the hardest split of the visible patches, and a neighbourhood consensus loss that aligns the scene similarities of both sensors. Extensive experiments on SAR-optical (BEN-14K, CBRSIR-VS) and PAN-MS (DSRSID) benchmarks demonstrate that RS-JEPA achieves the highest F1@5 among twelve re-implemented methods (72.92, 80.35 and 94.43) with a retrieval network of only 3.88M parameters (14.57M in training, 8-16times fewer than prior JEPA retrievers). Like prior methods, RS-JEPA trains on co-registered pairs. Our results highlight reading order as a source of self-supervised views. code: https://github.com/Tanishq251/RSJEPA.

[89] Measuring Robustness and Efficiency in a Connectome-Constrained Fly Visual System Model: Linking an Adversarial and a Perceptual Illusion cs.CV | q-bio.NCPDF

TL;DR: 论文在可微3D渲染的碰撞检测任务上，将受果蝇突触连接组约束的连续时间递归网络FlyNet与CNN、RAFT及无连接组约束的CTRNN进行对比，评估其鲁棒性与计算效率。研究发现FlyNet虽对对抗扰动脆弱，但相对其计算量对常见视频损坏比RAFT更鲁棒，且FLOPs可少达五个数量级。论文进一步将对抗纹理与reverse-phi错觉联系起来，指出二者可能利用同一低层运动统计量和电路脆弱性。

Details

Motivation: 生物视觉系统能在代谢和布线约束下可靠计算，而人工视觉系统能耗高且易受对抗扰动影响。论文关注受脑样连接组约束的网络是否能在更低计算成本下获得更好鲁棒性，并试图用连接组约束模型解释生物知觉脆弱性。

Insight: 创新点在于用连接组约束模型统一分析对抗扰动与reverse-phi知觉错觉，发现优化同一低层运动统计量即可诱导分类失败，从而将对抗失败机制转化为关于生物知觉脆弱性的可检验假设。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是将效率约束、对抗鲁棒性和生物错觉机制放在同一基准框架下比较，并通过反事实纹理实验排除“不熟悉纹理”这一简单解释。

Abstract: Biological visual systems compute reliably under tight metabolic and wiring constraints, whereas artificial vision systems consume far more energy and can be sensitive to adversarial perturbations. Whether networks with brain-like wiring are more robust at lower computational cost is unclear. We address this by benchmarking a task-optimized, continuous-time recurrent neural network of the fly visual system constrained by its synaptic connectome (FlyNet) against artificial neural networks. We compare it against CNNs, RAFT, and continuous-time recurrent neural networks without detailed connectome constraints (sCTRNN, CTRNN) on a collision-detection task within a differentiable 3D rendering pipeline. FlyNet is adversarially vulnerable but, relative to its compute, more robust to common video corruptions than RAFT, while using up to five orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs. The CTRNNs remain more robust than FlyNet across almost all tested perturbations, suggesting that connectome constraints benefit efficiency, through their low edge count, over robustness. Recurrence improves robustness to white-box attacks in RAFT and temporal context improves robustness to common corruptions. FlyNet’s position and velocity representations degrade under adversarial textures but recover under counterfactual textures that restore correct classification while staying equally far from training, ruling out unfamiliar textures as the cause of failure. The reverse-phi illusion similarly disrupts these representations and inverts the same low-level motion statistic as the adversarial textures. Optimizing this input statistic alone, without access to the network, suffices to induce classification failure, attributing both perturbations to the same circuit vulnerability. Connectome-constrained models can thus surface adversarial failure mechanisms as testable hypotheses about biological perceptual vulnerabilities.

[90] EarthRecord: From Pixels to Land Change with Multimodal Agents over Four Decades of Satellite Imagery cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Haoran Zhang, Huizhen Sun, Hanbin Wen, Jie Leng, Jiaxin Zhu

TL;DR: 论文提出 EarthRecord，一个基于 56 个站点、四十年原始多传感器卫星影像（Landsat、MODIS、VIIRS、Sentinel-2）的多模态智能体基准，要求智能体自行完成云掩膜和跨传感器协调，并回答 238 个土地变化问题、覆盖十类分析任务。评测 12 个多模态智能体发现，领先智能体能端到端完成多数分析，但在需要从影像映射答案时表现不佳，且答案仍可能跟随被篡改的卫星数据而非真实地面变化。

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态智能体虽能借助工具完成复杂数据分析，但其将数十年卫星影像转化为可靠土地变化分析的能力尚未被衡量；现有基准多基于精选图像或预处理输入，缺少从原始多传感器档案开始的评测。

Result: 在 EarthRecord 上评估 12 个多模态智能体，领先智能体可端到端完成多数分析任务，但在需要从影像制图的答案上明显失准；在仪器对控制中，当改变一个卫星的数据而地面不变时，约一半正确答案会跟随被改变的数据。

Insight: 创新点在于构建基于原始多传感器长时序影像的基准，并将云掩膜、跨传感器协调等预处理留给智能体；同时设计“闭卷”和“仪器对”控制实验，用于诊断智能体的答案究竟依赖先验知识、影像像素还是真实地面变化。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是以控制变量方式揭示多模态智能体在遥感时序分析中的可靠性边界。

Abstract: While multimodal agents now carry out complex data analysis with tools, their ability to turn decades of satellite imagery into reliable land-change analysis, crucial for Earth observation science, remains unmeasured. Existing benchmarks fall short: they pose questions on curated images or give agents preprocessed inputs, leaving out the raw multi-sensor archives where such analysis begins. We introduce EarthRecord, a benchmark built on the raw record of 56 sites: four decades of Landsat, MODIS, VIIRS, and Sentinel-2 data, with cloud masking and cross-sensor harmonization left to the agent. It poses 238 land-change questions across ten analysis tasks, scored against withheld references from global products, agreement among methods, and visual interpretation. Two controls test what answers rest on: the closed book removes the record, and instrument pairs alter one satellite’s data while nothing changes on the ground. Evaluations of twelve multimodal agents reveal that leading agents complete most analyses end to end but falter when an answer requires mapping from imagery. Further analysis shows that the record moves answers from prior knowledge to the pixels but not yet to the real change on the ground: when one satellite’s data are altered, half of the right answers follow the alteration. EarthRecord thus provides a principled platform for measuring and advancing agents from pixels to land change.

[91] Seeing Is Not Believing: Counterfactual Lesion Erasure Teaches a Small Medical Vision-Language Agent to Report Only What the Image Shows cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Bhaskar Gurram

TL;DR: 论文关注医学视觉语言模型会因问题中提及某病灶而报告该发现的问题，提出无需人工标注构建反事实视图（病灶擦除、同形状控制编辑、无病灶原图），并用证据敏感性差距ESG衡量模型是否真正依赖图像证据。作者训练小模型MedScope-4B，使其在病灶被擦除时更倾向回答不存在或转诊，在内部测试中显著优于多个前沿和开源VLM，但外部测试显示其真实病灶检测和罕见关键发现覆盖仍有限。

Details

Motivation: 医学VLM可能仅因问题命名了某发现就报告它，而标准基准未检测这种虚假报告。论文希望让模型只报告图像中确实存在的证据，抑制对已擦除或不存在的病灶作出肯定报告。

Result: 在507个内部案例、五类成像上，MedScope-4B取得ESG 0.758，擦除病灶后仍声称该病灶的比例为9.6%，优于前沿模型的ESG 0.27-0.47和41-62%，且真实病灶准确率无显著损失；无counterfactual views时ESG降至0.279±0.107，并在MedGemma 1.5 4B和两个未见inpainting工具上复现。外部中心优势仍保持，但前沿模型检测到更多真实病灶；新外部测试中MedScope-4B对48例气胸检测为0，净效用低于gemini-4-argon，且未建立相对removal-based DPO的稳定ESG优势。

Insight: 创新点在于利用公共分割数据集自动构造擦除、控制编辑和无病灶反事实视图，提出ESG指标，并用标签派生轨迹训练小模型形成“证据门控”倾向。客观看，反事实擦除评估与训练思路有借鉴价值，但论文也表明该方法不提升感知能力，且可能牺牲罕见关键发现的敏感性，需结合覆盖与检测能力改进。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) often report a finding because the question names it; standard benchmarks miss this. From public segmentation datasets we build counterfactual views without manual annotation: the lesion erased by inpainting, a same-shape control edit elsewhere, and unedited lesion-free images. The evidence sensitivity gap (ESG) is the rate of absent-or-refer answers after erasure minus the rate after the control edit; it is zero for a model that ignores the image or reacts to both edits alike. MedScope-4B (Qwen3.5-4B, LoRA, image tools) was trained on label-derived trajectories, selected by a pre-registered rule and tested once against nine frontier and six open VLMs; four pre-registered checks then probed the mechanism. On 507 internal cases across five imaging types, MedScope-4B reached ESG 0.758 and claimed an erased lesion in 9.6% of cases, against 0.27-0.47 and 41-62% for the frontier models (all p<0.001), without significant loss of accuracy on real lesions. Without counterfactual views ESG fell to 0.279+-0.107 (three seeds); the effect replicated on MedGemma 1.5 4B and two unseen inpainting tools. At four external centres the advantage held but frontier models detected more real lesions; on a fresh external test MedScope-4B detected 0 of 48 pneumothorax films, with net utility below gemini-4-argon’s (-0.36 [-0.50, -0.23]). Matching the control edit was not shown to matter; over three seeds no fresh-external ESG advantage over removal-based DPO was established; a paired-view consistency objective did not beat a matched-compute control, while that continuation alone cut erased-lesion claims on unseen tools at every seed, at a cost in sensitivity at one. Training on erased and edited images gives a small model an evidence-gated disposition that prompting does not give frontier models; it does not improve perception, and rare critical findings need coverage.

[92] Activation-Guided Graph Retrieval for Cognitive Memory Reconstruction in Language Model Systems cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.IRPDF

Marvin Danig

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向语言模型系统的激活引导图检索架构，用于重构认知记忆。系统先由请求与目标线索激活授权记忆图，再通过激活与扇出衰减分配有界遍历前沿，使检索实体递归发出线索，并在生成前重建类型化证据图。论文属于架构与分析型预印本，未报告基准测试结果，给出了成本估计、消融设计、失败条件和可复现评估协议。

Details

Motivation: 现有检索增强语言模型通常仅按查询相关性选择上下文，难以满足长期运行AI系统对记忆可用性的需求。论文主张引入“激活条件记忆可用性”作为第二操作符，使检索不仅返回排序列表，还能返回使记忆可用的证据路径。

Result: 论文未提供任何benchmark测量结果，仅给出实现级成本估计、消融实验设计、失败条件和可复现评估协议。其核心主张可被证伪：若更简单的图重排器在同等预算下能在因果与时间恢复上达到完整遍历效果，则该主张不成立。

Insight: 创新点在于将请求/目标线索、激活传播、扇出衰减、递归线索发射与类型化证据图重建统一为生成前的记忆重构流程，并总结候选选择、遍历控制、图正则化、证据重建和强化五方面架构后果。客观看，其价值更多在于提出可检验的架构假设与评估框架，而非已验证的性能提升。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented language model systems generally select context by query-conditioned relevance. We argue that long-lived AI systems require a second operator: activation-conditioned memory availability. We specify an architecture in which request and goal cues seed an authorized memory graph, activation and fan attenuation allocate a bounded traversal frontier, retrieved entities recursively emit cues, and a typed evidence graph is reconstructed before generation. The specification yields five architectural consequences about candidate selection, traversal control, graph regularization, evidence reconstruction, and reinforcement. We derive implementation-level cost estimates and define ablations, failure conditions, and a reproducible evaluation protocol. The central claim is narrowed or falsified if a simpler graph reranker matches full traversal on causal and temporal recovery under equal budgets. This architecture-and-analysis preprint reports no benchmark measurements. If the claim holds, retrieval returns the evidence path that makes a memory available, not only a ranked list.

[93] Agreeing on the Answer but Not the Reason: Measurement Limits of LLM Reasoning Failure Taxonomies cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Madhu Sudhan Rao Beesetty

TL;DR: 论文质疑将LLM推理失败分类法当作可靠测量的做法：作者定义了一个名为“组合崩溃”的失败类别（模型注意到问题的某个必需子部分，随后忽略或用错该部分），并在一个公开的多语言数学基准上，跨12个模型、11种语言、共2316条评分记录进行测量，发现报告出的失败比例在很大程度上取决于测量流程本身，包括标签测试顺序、prompt措辞以及基准数据缺陷，而不是单纯反映模型能力。

Details

Motivation: 当前许多论文把失败分类法的统计（某类错误占比、跨模型比较）当成客观测量来报告，但作者想检验这些比例是否真能反映模型推理能力的差异，还是主要由测量过程决定。

Result: 50.1%的失败样本同时带有多个类别的证据，改变分类器测试顺序会使“组合崩溃”的占比在26.4%到76.4%之间波动；母语者对答案正确性的判断与自动检查器一致，kappa=0.932，但对“组合崩溃”标签的一致性仅0.093；使用人类指令原样提示后，与人类标注的一致性kappa升至0.636；排除基准翻译缺陷项后，检查器标注该类别的频率从1.81倍降至1.17倍。

Insight: 创新点在于把失败分类法本身作为研究对象，系统揭示其标签比例的测量不可靠性，并提炼出四项低成本检查建议：报告标签共现、置换tie-break顺序、在共享独立样本上验证、审计系统性分歧，对LLM评估方法学具有普遍借鉴意义。

Abstract: Failure taxonomies for LLM reasoning are increasingly reported as measurements: papers state what fraction of a model’s errors fall into named categories, then compare those fractions across models. We show that for one such category the reported fraction is largely a property of the measurement procedure. We defined Compositional Collapse (a model notices a required sub-part of a problem, then drops or misapplies it) and measured it across twelve models and eleven languages on a public multilingual math benchmark (2,316 graded records). First, 50.1% of failures carry evidence for more than one category, so the reported plurality depends on the order in which the classifier tests them: reversing two tests moves it in eleven of twelve models, and across all six orderings Collapse’s share spans 26.4% to 76.4%. Second, agreement collapses for the category but not for the task. Native speakers of all eleven languages judged 179 transcripts, agreeing with our inspector at kappa = 0.932 on answer correctness but only kappa = 0.093 on Collapse. A second native speaker per language re-rated 142 items; on 121 determinate pairs the two humans agree with each other at kappa = 0.659, while each agrees with the inspector at kappa <= 0.094. Re-running the same inspector model with the human instructions verbatim raises kappa against Human 2 from 0.147 to 0.636, so the gap is largely attributable to the prompt, not the model. Third, the inspector labels Collapse 1.81 times as often as native speakers, but only 1.17 times once a defective benchmark item (a deleted clause in the Bengali and Chinese translations) is excluded. We recommend four cheap checks before comparing failure-mode rates across models: report label co-occurrence, permute the tie-break, validate on a shared independent sample, and audit systematic disagreements.

[94] Darmok: Championship Cryptic Crossword Solving via Constraint Tightening and Resilient Orchestration cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.DCPDF

Siddhartha Chatterjee

TL;DR: 论文提出Darmok，首个在英国 cryptic crossword 上达到超人类水平的自动求解系统。该系统在55届《泰晤士报》填字锦标赛中无错误解出全部七道谜题，每轮均快于所有人类选手，决赛比人类冠军快3分13秒。系统通过多模型交叉验证、约束收紧和弹性编排实现高准确率、速度与可靠性。

Details

Motivation: 英国 cryptic crossword 线索隐晦、网格稀疏且比赛规则要求零错误，现有前沿AI模型虽平均表现好但云API不稳定（超时、拒绝、循环），需兼顾比赛级准确率、速度与最坏情况下的可靠性。

Result: 在55届《泰晤士报》填字锦标赛中，Darmok无错误解出全部七题，每轮均快于所有人类选手，决赛用时6分52秒，领先人类冠军3分13秒；五个月日常评估中保持近零错误。

Insight: 创新点包括：三轮三模型交叉检查线索、利用已验证交叉字母解锁难题、整谜题模型与字典查找兜底且不覆盖已确认词。客观分析认为其核心贡献在于强调复杂AI系统需从平均情况优化转向最坏情况弹性，备份路径在模型严重降速时保障求解。

Abstract: We present Darmok, the first automated system to reach superhuman performance on British cryptic crosswords. Unlike American-style crosswords, cryptic clues disguise an unmarked definition and compositional wordplay beneath a misleading surface reading, sparse grids leave nearly half of each word’s letters unchecked, and tournament rules rank a grid with even a single wrong or blank square behind all error-free solutions regardless of speed. Competing alongside an elite human field at the 55th Times Crossword Championship in London, Darmok solved all seven championship puzzles error-free and finished faster than every human competitor in every round. It solved the Final puzzle in 6m 52s, a full 3m 13s ahead of the human champion. Today’s frontier AI models can on average solve a whole puzzle in a single try with comparable accuracy and speed. However, cloud API calls still stall, time out, trigger safety refusals, or get stuck in reasoning loops. To simultaneously achieve tournament-grade accuracy, speed, and reliability, Darmok cross-checks clues across three independent models in rounds; uses verified crossing letters to unlock harder clues; and uses a whole-puzzle model and dictionary lookups to fill any remaining gaps without overwriting confirmed words. This design maintained near-zero-error performance across a five-month daily evaluation period. Under normal benchmark conditions, removing backup models, fallback steps, or elaborate prompt instructions barely changes accuracy, making the architecture appear over-engineered. Yet during both the worst days of daily operation and a severe slowdown of our primary model in the live Championship Final, the backup paths carried the solve, showing that as foundation models master the average case, the central challenge in building complex AI systems shifts to resilience in the worst case.

[95] Towards Reliable VLM Judges: State-Conditional Invariance and Presentation-Aware Diagnostics cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Qinan Zhang, Xinyu Wang, Qihang Jin, Tianyuan Wang, Hongbing Luo

TL;DR: 论文关注视觉语言模型作为多模态与具身任务自动评判者时的可靠性问题，提出状态条件不变性（SCI）原则：评判者应在语义保持的呈现变化下稳定，同时对改变任务结果的因果干预作出响应。作者构建了基于确定性MiniGrid轨迹的模拟器诊断基准GridWM-Judge，包含完整、移除证据、反事实等变体及呈现/表示探针，并在五类诊断任务上评估局部感知、动作条件转移、呈现稳定性、表示可交换性和反事实因果判别。

Details

Motivation: 现有VLM评判者常以聚合准确率或一致性衡量，但单一分数无法判断模型是否因正确原因改变判断；论文旨在区分语义保持变化下的不稳定与对因果干预不敏感等失败模式，推动更可靠的VLM评判。

Result: 论文未报告具体数值，但诊断评估显示VLM评判者存在异构失败模式，包括对证据呈现和编码敏感、成功轨迹上激活低、配对反事实判别弱；感知控制分析表明视觉 grounding 与目标恢复是显著且依赖模型的瓶颈。

Insight: 主要创新是提出SCI可靠性原则并将其操作化为GridWM-Judge受控诊断基准，系统测试呈现稳定性、表示可交换性与因果敏感性；客观看，其价值在于将评判可靠性从准确率/一致性扩展到状态条件不变性与因果判别，并主张联合报告正确性、呈现稳定性和因果敏感性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges for multimodal and embodied tasks, yet aggregate accuracy or consistency alone does not reveal whether a judge changes its verdict for the right reason. We propose State-Conditional Invariance (SCI), a reliability principle requiring a judge to remain stable under semantics-preserving changes in presentation while responding to causal interventions that change the task outcome. We instantiate SCI in GridWM-Judge, a simulator-grounded diagnostic benchmark built from deterministic MiniGrid trajectories with aligned Full, evidence-removal, and counterfactual variants, plus presentation and representation probes. Across five diagnostic tasks, GridWM-Judge tests local perception, action-conditioned transitions, presentation stability, representation exchangeability, and Full-CF causal discrimination. Our diagnostic evaluations reveal heterogeneous failure modes, including sensitivity to evidence presentation and encoding, low activation on successful trajectories, and weak paired counterfactual discrimination. Perception-controlled analyses further indicate that visual grounding and objective recovery are substantial, model-dependent bottlenecks. These results motivate jointly reporting correctness, presentation stability, and causal sensitivity instead of relying on a single score.

[96] Breakdown of Autoregressive Multi-Step Reasoning Under Concentrated vs. Diffuse Attention Head Dropout cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Karol Struniawski

TL;DR: 论文研究自回归大语言模型在多步推理中对注意力头缺失的鲁棒性，比较集中爆发的注意力头掩码与预算匹配的弥散随机dropout。作者在GSM8K上对Qwen3-8B和LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct进行250组配对推导评估，发现集中式扰动会显著损害推理链，而将相同预算均匀分散到生成步骤时退化更轻。

Details

Motivation: 受哺乳动物清醒时新皮层局部短暂“off-state”神经元沉默现象启发，论文关注时空集中的结构扰动如何影响LLM的序列推理，并试图为Transformer架构的容错性提供实证基准。

Result: 在GSM8K上，集中burst dropout使Qwen3-8B从弥散噪声下的91.2%降至78.8%（-12.4pp），LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct从84.0%降至76.8%（-7.2pp）；将相同累计预算按分层五分位均匀分散后，Qwen和LLaMA分别保持91.2%和84.0%。误差分析显示Qwen主要体现为格式化响应内的推导错误，而LLaMA部分不一致损失缺少最终答案格式行。

Insight: 创新点在于将神经科学中的局部瞬态沉默类比为LLM注意力头的集中式掩码，并严格控制dropout预算比较集中与弥散扰动的差异。客观上看，论文揭示了自回归推理链对结构性集中扰动的脆弱性，以及扰动预算在生成步骤上的分布方式会显著影响模型容错表现。

Abstract: During extended wakefulness, mammalian neocortical networks experience localized periods of transient neuronal silence termed off-states. Here, we examine how analogous spatiotemporally concentrated disruptions affect sequential reasoning in autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs). Evaluating 250 paired derivations on the GSM8K benchmark across two 8B models (Qwen3-8B and LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct), we contrast clustered bursts of attention head masking against budget-matched diffuse i.i.d. dropout. Concentrated bursts induce substantial reasoning degradation: at onset parameter $\mathcal{B}=0.10$ (scheduling $\sim 5.7%$ of vulnerable head-token volume), Qwen3-8B accuracy drops from $91.2%$ under diffuse noise to $78.8%$ under burst dropout ($Δ= -12.4$ percentage points [pp], exact McNemar $p = 5.5 \times 10^{-6}$), while LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct drops from $84.0%$ to $76.8%$ ($Δ= -7.2$ pp, $p = 0.0198$). Conversely, dispersing the identical cumulative budget uniformly across generation steps via stratified quintile matching yields substantially milder degradation (retaining $91.2%$ in Qwen and $84.0%$ in LLaMA). In an exploratory factorial dissociation ($N=100$), single-step dropout density is associated with an acute drop in a single schedule draw ($-10.0$ pp on Qwen, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.0425$), while multi-token continuity exhibits an inconclusive compounding trend ($-7.0$ pp, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.1892$). Error analysis reveals that Qwen failures reflect derivation errors within formatted responses (conditional accuracy drop of $-11.4$ pp), whereas in LLaMA $22.2%$ of discordant losses lack a final answer format line (conditional gap narrows to $-5.0$ pp). These findings demonstrate that autoregressive reasoning chains are particularly vulnerable to concentrated structural disruptions, providing empirical benchmarks for fault tolerance in transformer architectures.

[97] Multimodal Abstention That Preserves Acquired Correct Answers cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Jinyuan Zhang

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态模型在任务适配后加入拒答能力时，容易丢失原本已答对的问题及适配阶段获得的能力。作者将拒答修复形式化为：在限制新丢失正确答案数量的预算下，最大化对不可回答问题正确拒答。提出 ReFrain，结合首 token logits 线性探针、在留出图像簇上的阈值校准和确定性否决机制，将拒答输出替换为“以上都不是”，同时保留其他回答。

Details

Motivation: 任务适配后的多模态模型需要能对不可回答问题进行拒答，但直接加入拒答会误伤已答对样本，包括 SFT 适配获得的能力；因此需要在尽量不损失正确答案的约束下提升正确拒答率。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-3B 上，零预算时 ReFrain 保留全部 57 个 SFT 增益答案且无损失，正确拒答率提升 7.30 个百分点；0.5% 校准预算下达到 74.05% 正确拒答，仅损失 8 个、保留 55 个增益，优于 CLIP-UP EmbLoRA 的 64.65%。独立 228 个问题簇确认实验中，0.5% 点达到 66.05% 正确拒答且无损失，超过 EmbLoRA 32.72 个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于把拒答修复建模为带“新丢失正确答案预算”的优化问题，并通过首 token logits 探针、图像簇阈值校准和确定性 veto 将拒答与原始回答修改解耦，从而在提升拒答能力的同时显式保护已获得的正确答案。

Abstract: Adding abstention to a task-adapted multimodal model can discard correct answers, including capabilities acquired during adaptation. We formulate abstention repair as maximizing correct abstention on unanswerable questions under a budget on newly lost correct answers. We propose ReFrain (Regression-Free Abstention), which combines a linear probe on first-token logits, threshold calibration on held-out image clusters, and a deterministic veto that replaces rejected outputs with the displayed “none of the above” option while preserving all other responses. “Regression-free” denotes zero loss on the calibration set at the zero-budget target. All abstention methods in our main comparison start from the same answering-SFT checkpoint; we separately track lost correct answers, repaired errors, and retained SFT gains. On Qwen2.5-VL-3B, ReFrain at zero budget retains all 57 SFT-gained answers with no losses and improves correct abstention by 7.30 percentage points. At a 0.5% calibration budget, it reaches 74.05% correct abstention with 8 losses and retains 55 gains; CLIP-UP EmbLoRA reaches 64.65% with 62 losses, 41 repairs, and 40 retained gains. The same probe at its default threshold loses 298 correct answers. With models and thresholds frozen, confirmation on 228 independent question clusters yields 66.05% correct abstention and no losses at the 0.5% point, exceeding EmbLoRA by 32.72 percentage points. Ordinary refusal SFT achieves higher correct abstention at greater regression cost. Code and reproduction materials: https://github.com/ModeLiberaL/ReFrain.

[98] GARD: Gradient-Guided Adversarial Representation Defense against Backdoors in Multimodal Contrastive Learning Models cs.CR | cs.CVPDF

Yuhong Wang, Yujun Cai, Lei Feng, Songze Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 GARD，一种面向 CLIP 等多模态对比学习模型的后门防御方法。它利用全局与局部对抗扰动探针最大化位移干净图像表示，再将扰动表示与分离的干净锚点对齐，同时保持图文对齐。该方法不需要估计攻击目标或触发器，且推理时无额外计算。

Details

Motivation: 多模态对比学习模型易受多样化触发器后门攻击，现有干净微调可能残留后门，触发器反演方法又依赖准确估计攻击目标和触发器。论文观察到后门编码器存在结构相关的表示敏感性：全局扰动更易暴露分布式后门，局部块扰动更易暴露局部后门。

Result: 在六种视觉后门攻击和两种 CLIP 视觉骨干的 12 个攻击-骨干组合上，GARD 将攻击成功率降至 1% 以下，同时保持有竞争力的干净准确率。

Insight: 创新点在于提出目标与触发器无关的后处理防御，利用全局/局部互补对抗表示探针揭示并修复后门表示，无需触发器反演且不增加推理开销。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是利用表示敏感性差异进行后门检测与修复。

Abstract: Multimodal contrastive learning models such as CLIP are vulnerable to backdoor attacks with diverse trigger structures, ranging from localized patches to globally distributed perturbations, with recent attacks increasingly optimizing these triggers in the representation space. Existing post-hoc defenses based on clean fine-tuning may leave backdoors active, while trigger-inversion methods depend on accurately estimating the attack target and trigger. We observe that backdoored encoders exhibit structure-dependent representation sensitivity: global perturbations preferentially expose distributed backdoors, whereas local patch perturbations are more effective for localized backdoors. Based on this observation, we propose Gradient-Guided Adversarial Representation Defense (GARD), a target- and trigger-agnostic post-hoc defense. GARD optimizes complementary global and local adversarial probes to maximally displace clean-image representations, and then realigns the perturbed representations with detached clean anchors while preserving image-text alignment. The resulting model requires no additional computation at inference time. Across six visual backdoor attacks and two CLIP visual backbones, GARD reduces the attack success rate below 1% in all 12 evaluated attack-backbone combinations while maintaining competitive clean accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of adversarial representation probing for repairing backdoored multimodal contrastive encoders.

[100] Topology-Aware Reinforcement Learning for CAD Decomposition toward All-Hexahedral Meshing cs.GR | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Jiaming Peng, Zhihao Zheng, Rui Wang, Qinglin Wang, Jing Xiao

TL;DR: 论文提出DecompRL，一个面向全六面体网格生成的CAD分解深度强化学习框架。针对复杂CAD模型中正交或交互特征错位导致直接六面体网格不可行的问题，方法设计拓扑感知的内部切割动作，并结合几何奖励与可网格化反馈来学习有效分解。实验在ABC、Fusion360、NIST和DeepCAD上验证，在多数基准上取得较高成功率并保持有竞争力的网格质量。

Details

Motivation: 现有CAD分解方法（包括框架场流水线和学习型方法）常受几何与拓扑复杂性影响，且动作空间多局限于表面分割，容易产生无效或不可网格化的分解，难以处理具有错位特征的复杂CAD模型。

Result: 在ABC、Fusion360、NIST、DeepCAD四个基准中，DecompRL在三个基准上取得所评估学习型方法中的最高成功率；Fusion360上成功率提升最大，达到59.6%，同时网格质量具有竞争力。其分解运行时间相比完整LMFF流程最多快两个数量级（不含后续网格生成）。

Abstract: Automatic hexahedral meshing for complex CAD models has long been regarded as the “holy grail” of mesh generation. The fundamental challenge arises from feature misalignment, where orthogonal or interacting features coexist, making direct hexahedral meshing infeasible and requiring decomposition into simpler, meshable blocks. While existing decomposition methods, including frame-field pipelines and learning-based techniques, have reduced manual intervention, they can remain sensitive to geometric and topological complexity and may fail to produce valid decompositions for challenging feature configurations. Specifically, their action spaces are typically restricted to surface-based splitting, which often yields invalid or unmeshable decompositions. To address these limitations, we propose DecompRL, a deep reinforcement learning framework for CAD decomposition toward hexahedral meshing. We design a set of topology-aware internal-splitting actions that leverage both global topological structure and local geometric property to effectively separate misaligned features. Furthermore, we introduce geometric rewards together with meshability feedback to guide the learning process toward valid decompositions and high-quality meshes. Extensive experiments on ABC, Fusion360, NIST, and DeepCAD show that DecompRL achieves the highest success rate among the evaluated learning-based methods on three of the four benchmarks, while maintaining competitive mesh quality. The measured LMFF-to-DecompRL runtime ratio reaches up to two orders of magnitude; this compares the full LMFF procedure with DecompRL decomposition alone, excluding subsequent mesh generation. The largest SR improvement is observed on Fusion360, where DecompRL reaches a success rate of 59.6%.

[101] TypedInk: Knowing When Not To Draw cs.HC | cs.CVPDF

Weiwei Yang

TL;DR: 论文提出 TypedInk，一个面向科研插图的类型化生成框架：它从可能不完整的科研记录中推断应生成 diagram、plot 还是 plate，并仅在满足对应“证据契约”时才调度渲染器，否则拒绝绘图。在 TypedFigBench 上，TypedInk 能拒绝缺少表格的视图以避免编造数字，并标记仅有意图的视图为示意性；而 always-draw 基线会无条件生成图表并产生零金标数字恢复。

Details

Motivation: 现有完整源插图生成器默认输入完整，但真实科研记录常只有提纲、段落、表格或一句意图的任意组合；若缺少表格却用图像模型绘制测量数值，就会产生幻觉结果。论文关注生成器何时应因证据不足而拒绝绘图。

Result: 在 TypedFigBench 的 6 个 gold plot（5 种视图，n=30）上，TypedInk 在有表格时 12/12 复现全部 gold 表格 token，拒绝 12 个隐藏表格视图，并将 6 个仅意图视图标为示意性且无执行失败；always-draw 基线生成 30/30 图表（含 18 个无表格视图）但恢复零金标数字，视觉语言评分器仍将其判为可提交 17/36 次，而 TypedInk 的拒绝被误判为可提交 0/36 次。类型路由消融显示仅给提纲时类型准确率 0.31，所有 gold plot 均触发完整性违规；聊天模型规划器提升至 0.85 但仍有 25% 提纲完整性失败。

Insight: 创新点在于将科研插图生成形式化为带类型（diagram/plot/plate）和证据契约的调度与拒答问题，而非无条件绘图；通过类型路由与完整性约束显式区分“可画”与“证据不足不可画”，并用诊断消融定位失败来源，强调拒绝生成是避免科学幻觉的关键能力。

Abstract: Can a scientific figure generator know when it does not have enough evidence to draw? Complete-source illustrators map a finished methodology section to a schematic, or a table to plotting code, and are never asked this question because their input is assumed complete. The research record is rarely that complete: an outline, a draft paragraph, a table, or a one-sentence intent, in any combination, and painting measured numbers with an image model when the table is missing is a hallucinated result. TypedInk infers a figure type from {diagram, plot, plate} from a possibly incomplete source and dispatches to the renderer that can honor that type’s evidence contract, refusing dispatch when the contract cannot be met. On the six gold plots in TypedFigBench (5 views, n=30), TypedInk’s code path inks every gold table token when a table is observed (12/12), refuses the 12 hidden-table views instead of inventing numbers, and marks 6 intent-only views illustrative, with no execution failures. An always-draw coder with no such gate inks 30/30 charts, including all 18 with no table, and recovers zero gold numbers on any of them; two vision-language raters (not humans; human replication is future work) still judge those always-draw charts submittable 17/36 times, against TypedInk’s refusal 0/36 times. A diagnostic field ablation of the type router (16 illustrations, 5 views each, n=80) locates where this failure would originate if unchecked: an outline alone yields type accuracy 0.31 and sends every gold plot into an integrity violation, and a chat-model planner narrows but does not close the gap (overall accuracy 0.85, still integrity-failing on 25% of outlines). Diagram-label recall is 0.47 on the full record and 0.19 on an outline. Gold plates are typed at dispatch (oracle integrity-fail 0.00) and rendered as stills.

Details

Motivation: 多轮OPD中，让教师模型参考同任务成功轨迹可提供 hindsight，但不同成功轨迹可能到达不兼容的交互状态，从而给出误导性监督。论文旨在解决如何为每条失败轨迹选择最兼容的成功参考，以提升多轮工具使用智能体的蒸馏效果。

Result: 在ALFWorld和τ³-bench的airline域上，LCS是唯一在ALFWorld总体分数和airline全部三项指标上均优于无参考OPD的参考选择器；无需进一步优化，airline训练的模型迁移到retail域后，LCS也在三项指标上均有提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-inference distribution mismatch by supervising a student on its own trajectories. For multi-turn tool-use agents, conditioning the teacher on a validated same-task successful trajectory as a reference can provide useful hindsight, but different successes may reach incompatible interaction states and offer misleading supervision. We cast successful-reference selection as a free-target optimal transport problem, which reduces to assigning each failed trajectory its most compatible successful reference. Because latent state paths are hidden, we use action-sequence alignment as an observable proxy for compatibility and instantiate it with longest common subsequence (LCS), which identifies a shared ordered backbone of actions while allowing recoverable detours. This perspective makes trajectory compatibility the central criterion for reference selection in multi-turn OPD. Experiments on ALFWorld and the airline domain of $τ^3$-bench show that LCS is the only evaluated reference selector to improve over reference-free OPD on both ALFWorld overall scores and all three airline metrics. Without further optimization, the airline-trained models also transfer to the retail domain, where LCS improves all three metrics.
