Motivation: 现有影响估计多停留在单样本层面，难以刻画某模态在不同样本间冗余/有用差异以及样本与模态交互；论文旨在跨样本、跨模态地度量模态对多模态学习结果的贡献。

Result: 在两个多模态基准（卫星图像、道路网络、文本、音频、表格数据等）上，SKS与真实预测结果的相关性比现有方法高57.8%；下游数据选择平均准确率提升1.93%，且所选数据训练的模型Hessian迹更低、损失面更平坦。

Insight: 创新点在于将跨样本模态归因建模为从样本/模态选择状态到联合训练预测结果的结构化代理映射，并用组合核显式编码模态对相似性与样本对相似性的乘积交互；客观看，其可借鉴之处是结构化核、代理模型与梯度估计结合的高效归因框架及面向数据选择的验证。

Abstract: We study the problem of cross-sample modality attribution, which involves measuring how one modality in one sample affects the learning outcome in other multimodal samples. This problem is crucial in a multimodal system because a data modality (such as a text description) may be redundant for one sample (a self-explanatory image), but the same data modality would otherwise be uniquely informative for another sample, or informative when combined with other modalities. Prior work focuses on influence estimation at the level of one sample, and it remains an open question how one should capture the interactions across both samples and modalities. To capture scenarios like the above, we propose structured kernel surrogate models, which are surrogate mappings from both samples and modalities to the prediction outcome when they are trained together. We design an algorithm, SKS, which applies kernel ridge regression with a structured, compositional kernel on the selection states of individual samples and modalities. Specifically, the kernel function multiplies kernel-based similarity scores between every pair of data modalities for one sample and a pair of two samples. Additionally, we design a gradient-based estimation algorithm to efficiently estimate the structured kernel surrogate model. We evaluate our approach on two multimodal benchmarks, spanning a range of data modalities including satellite images, road networks, text, audio, and tabular data. We find that SKS achieves $57.8$% higher correlation with the true prediction outcomes than existing methods. In downstream data selection, SKS improves the average accuracy by $1.93$% over baselines. Models with data selected by SKS have lower Hessian traces, indicating a flatter loss surface. Using SKS to select satellite images identifies road segments, where accident scenes commonly occur, as opposed to background images.

[106] MicroLens: Read, Trace, and Rewire the Residual Stream with Two Lenses cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.HCPDF

Nika Zahedi, Fangyi Zhu, Anna Wu

TL;DR: 论文提出交互式查看器 MicroLens，在残差流的“层×token”网格上统一支持读取、追溯和编辑：每个单元可用 logit lens 与 Jacobian lens（J-lens）解码，追溯其来源 token，并编辑后即时重跑模型。作者在 Gemma-3-4B-it 上用数百条 prompt 展示三个使用场景，揭示双 lens 的编辑方向差异、两跳问答中早期信息可读与因果重定向，以及推理前答案已在残差流中可见等现象。

Details

Motivation: 现有将隐藏状态解码为词的热图只能说明向量“读起来像什么”，无法区分模型是否以及多大程度使用了该信息。论文希望提供统一工具来同时读取、追踪和编辑残差流，从而更可靠地分析模型内部信息流。

Result: 在 Gemma-3-4B-it 和数百条 prompt 扫描上：logit lens 方向适合设置 token 输出，J-lens 方向适合改变后续 token 读取；两跳 trivia 中城市 token 的州/国家信息最早第 7 层可读，替换可 92-98% 重定向答案；逐步推理前正确答案已是残差流 top word 的比例为 retrieval 89-92%、multi-hop/serial 39-63%，远高于干扰项最多 5%，但 ordering puzzles 例外；J-lens 通常比 logit lens 早一两层读到可见答案。

Insight: 创新点在于把双 lens 解码、token 溯源、编辑与即时重跑集成到同一残差流网格中，形成可交互的机制分析工具。客观来看，其价值在于用双 lens 区分“可读出”与“被使用”，并通过因果替换和跨问题类型扫描展示早期信息可读性及 J-lens 的提前读取优势。

Abstract: Lenses that decode hidden states into words are a popular way to look inside language models, but a heatmap of decoded words only shows what a vector reads as and cannot distinguish whether, or how much, the model uses the information. We present an interactive viewer that puts reading, tracing and editing on the same layers-by-tokens grid of the residual stream. In the viewer, every cell can be decoded with two lenses, the logit lens and the Jacobian lens (J-lens), traced back to the tokens it drew from, and edited, with the rest of the model rerun on the spot. We demonstrate three usage scenarios on Gemma-3-4B-it, backed by sweeps over a few hundred prompts, to show what this tool can discover. First, the two lenses give different directions to write with, each suiting a different edit: the logit lens’s word direction sets what a token outputs, while the J-lens’s direction changes what later tokens read. Second, in two-hop trivia questions (the capital of the state or country containing a given city), the state or country is readable at the city token from as early as layer 7, and swapping it there can redirect the answer 92-98% of the time; however, at the answer position, the state or country information is not used. Third, when a chat model answers questions with a step-by-step reasoning prompt, the correct answer is often already the top word in the residual stream before any reasoning is written: for 89-92% of retrieval questions and 39-63% of multi-hop and serial ones, far above their respective distractors (at most 5%). However, ordering puzzles are an exception, as the model works them out only once it writes its final reasoning steps. Furthermore, for answers that are visible in the residual stream, the J-lens reads them a layer or two earlier than the logit lens across the different types of question.

[107] Free Lunch Uncertainty: Sequence-Level Rényi Entropy from Particle Decoding for Adaptive LLM Reasoning cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo, Milad Heidari Sorkhehdizaj, Shahab Rahimirad, Aliasghar Pourghani, Seyedarmin Azizi

TL;DR: 论文提出利用SMC粒子解码/power sampling中已经估计的配分函数，以零边际成本得到LLM completion分布的序列级Rényi熵（SRE），并推广到任意阶的逐步熵族。论文将1/2阶SRE用于选择性预测和自适应推理算力分配，并在MATH500上验证其优于log-prob等零成本信号，同时提出基于粒子探针的两阶段策略以减少粒子消耗。

Details

Motivation: 现有mean token entropy、log-prob等零成本不确定性信号只关注token或单个completion，而self-consistency需要额外多次采样；论文希望在不增加采样成本的情况下，为选择性预测和自适应计算提供整段LLM输出的序列级不确定性度量。

Result: 1/2阶SRE在13个设置中的10个成为最佳零成本选择性预测信号；在MATH500上，其AUROC在四个模型及长度分位内均比log-prob高0.022到0.044。8粒子探针可识别受益于更多粒子的问题，两阶段策略在三个随机种子下匹配64粒子精度并将粒子数减少34.2%。

Insight: 创新点在于从SMC power sampling的正常数估计中“免费”读取序列级Rényi熵，并用Rényi链式法则扩展到逐步熵族；论文证明高阶熵与每步最可能token相关，而1/2阶熵累积每步搜索努力。客观来看，其价值在于把粒子解码的内部诊断量转化为不确定性估计和自适应计算信号，并给出任意粒子预算下的保证。

Abstract: Quantifying uncertainty over an entire LLM completion is critical for selective prediction and adaptive compute. Zero-cost signals such as mean token entropy and log-probability are local to tokens or to one completion, and self-consistency needs many extra completions. We show that Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) decoding for power sampling already estimates the normalizing constant of the power distribution, which gives the sequence-level R’enyi entropy (SRE) of the model’s completion distribution at zero marginal cost, with a guarantee at every particle budget. A R’enyi chain rule extends this readout to a family of stepwise entropies of any order. We prove that orders above one are tied to the most likely token of each step, which explains why they track log-probability, while the order-1/2 member accumulates the log of per-step search effort. This member is the best zero-cost selective-prediction signal in 10 of 13 settings. On MATH500 its AUROC is 0.022 to 0.044 above log-probability with all four models and within length quartiles, and the same order is selected when each setting is held out. An 8-particle probe identifies the problems that benefit from more particles, and a two-pass policy matches 64-particle accuracy on MATH500 with 34.2% fewer particles over three seeds.

[109] HaiRuo: Forecasting Ocean Eddies as Fields and Objects with a Satellite-Altimetry Foundation Model cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Xukun Qin, Haochen Sun, Yuyang Tian, Ge Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 HaiRuo，一个面向海洋中尺度涡的预报框架，可同时以”场”和”对象”两种形式进行预报：输入30天的卫星海面高度图，单次前向即可输出未来15天的海面高度与地转流场、每日涡旋掩码，以及当前每个涡旋的完整轨迹。该框架由两部分组成：在24年全球卫星测高无标签数据上预训练的10.5亿参数基础模型 HaiRuo-1B，以及采用拉格朗日视角、在涡旋随动极坐标框架下进行多轮前瞻细化的对象解码器 XuanJi。

Details

Abstract: Mesoscale eddies carry most of the ocean’s kinetic energy and move heat, salt and nutrients across basins, yet where an eddy will be two weeks ahead is still read off ocean models with post-hoc tracking heuristics. We introduce HaiRuo, which forecasts eddies as fields and as objects at once: from 30 days of satellite sea-level maps, one forward pass returns 15 days of sea level and geostrophic velocity, daily eddy masks and the trajectory of every eddy alive today. HaiRuo pairs two components with a clear division of labour: (1) HaiRuo-1B, an open-weight 1.05-billion-parameter foundation model pretrained without labels on 24 years of global satellite-altimetry observations, which supplies the 15-day physical fields and masks (+0.14 field skill and +1.1 mIoU points under the same decoder) and further lowers 15-day centre error by 1.2 km frozen (1.35 km over training from scratch); and (2) XuanJi, a Lagrangian object decoder that samples the evolving forecast in each eddy’s co-moving polar frame and refines the 15-day path across three look-ahead rounds, removing 2.5 km on HaiRuo-1B (2.9 km with joint fine-tuning) and 2.5-3.3 km via its object attention across six published backbones. Under one pre-registered global evaluation over the whole ocean, HaiRuo improves on fourteen methods on every endpoint (25.2 vs. 30.6 km 15-day centre error, -18%), holding its margin outside the labelled regions and through both phases of the El Nino-Southern Oscillation (ENSO), while reproducing westward drift, 50-60 deg S eastward reversal and poleward cyclone-anticyclone deflection. Over ten days through one fixed tracker, HaiRuo’s forecast field cuts the eddy-centre error of WenHai and XiHe by 37-43% (-2.8 km vs. day 0 while they degrade +4.3-8.7 km), and its object trajectories cut pipeline error by 77-78% globally. Open-weight checkpoints, code and project page: https://github.com/xkqin/HaiRuo

Details

Abstract: System-1 Decision Models directly make structured probabilistic decisions over query-specified options given a state in a single forward pass, without autoregressively generating reasoning trajectories or textual responses. Recent evaluations cover diverse tasks but often rely on existing benchmarks, where prior exposure to test material can obscure generalization. Moreover, real-world decision making often involves long contexts, large option spaces, and complex logical dependencies, challenges that existing benchmarks do not adequately capture together. Thus, we introduce MMDM (Massive Multitask Decision Making), a benchmark of 5,448 high-quality questions across 16 task categories, combining newly constructed tasks with source-grounded recompositions and rewrites for single-select, multi-select, and soft-label decision making in fresh contexts. It covers realistic scenarios such as agent state tracking, tool and skill selection, preference judgments, and rubric scoring, with option spaces ranging from 2 to 1,024 and inputs extending to 64,817 tokens. With MMDM, we conduct a comprehensive quantitative evaluation of 28 decision models, showing that many perform well on common knowledge yet struggle with interdependent options, constrained decisions, and multi-select tasks. For example, Perplexity Decider-V1.2 reaches 78.75% Hard Single-select Accuracy on world knowledge, but only 53.95% on option logic and 47.95% on constraint planning, while the best Hard Multi-select Accuracy across all models is 36.81%. Zijian Zhang, Rizhen Hu, Jiangweizhi Peng, Shiyang Li, Sujay Bhatt

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoScaffold，一种无需人工干预的 AI-for-AI 框架：利用冻结教师模型周期性分析学生模型的强化学习训练经验，诊断瓶颈，并生成跨任务策略与难任务提示两类自适应脚手架，训练后评估时移除脚手架。该方法应用于 ALFWorld、搜索式问答、数学推理和 GPU kernel 生成，并在四个领域均优于对应 RL 基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) produces rich training experience, yet outcome rewards reduce each attempt to a single number and leave most of this information unused. Training-time scaffolds, such as strategies or hints added to the prompt during training, offer a natural way to turn this information into guidance, but their design and adaptation are largely left to humans and tailored to specific domains. We introduce AutoScaffold, a unified AI-for-AI framework that turns a student’s training experience into adaptive scaffolds, with minimal domain-specific information and no human intervention during training. A frozen teacher periodically examines this experience, diagnoses bottlenecks, and designs scaffolds in two forms: (i) strategies shared across a class of tasks and (ii) hints for individual hard tasks, adjusting the content and amount of support as the student learns. The scaffolds are removed at evaluation. We apply the same framework to ALFWorld, search-based QA, mathematical reasoning, and GPU kernel generation. When evaluated without scaffolds, students trained with AutoScaffold outperform those trained with the corresponding RL baselines in all four domains, with gains of 7.2 points in ALFWorld success and 4.0 points in average QA exact match. On GPU kernel generation, AutoScaffold increases the fraction of tasks with a correct kernel that runs faster than the PyTorch reference by up to 5.0 points. On math, our student surpasses QuestA’s reported accuracy using approximately one-third as many student training steps. Analyses show that the teacher identifies a different failure in each task class and shifts its scaffolds from step-by-step workflows to targeted fixes as the student improves, and that its revised scaffolds help the student learn more than keeping the previous scaffold or using no scaffold.

TL;DR: 论文提出莫比乌斯交互滤波（MoIF），用于多模态幽默检测。方法通过私有时间编码器和平衡互惠交互构建单模态、双模态、三模态预测器，并将其统一聚合结构解释为Möbius域滤波器，从而对不同阶交互响应实现几何递减的阶选择谱正则化。这样既保留高阶多模态表示，又调节其对最终预测的贡献。

Expressive fusion must also regulate how interactions of different orders enter the final judgment. We propose Möbius Interaction Filtering (MoIF), which learns a modality-subset prediction function and imposes order-selective spectral regularization through its aggregation structure. Private temporal encoders and balanced reciprocal interaction construct supervised unimodal, bimodal, and trimodal predictors. Their uniform aggregate is exactly a Möbius-domain filter with a geometrically decreasing interaction-order response. This formulation retains higher-order representations while regulating their contribution to prediction. Controlled UR-FUNNY perturbations reveal a pronounced decline in label-aligned signal-to-noise ratio across interaction orders. The induced filter improves the final ratio by 1.62-2.79 times relative to the co-trained trimodal predictor while preserving its own clean class-aligned signal under perturbation. Five-seed experiments achieve 70.6% accuracy and weighted F1 on UR-FUNNY, exceeding thirteen reproduced methods on these detection metrics. CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI evaluations establish applicability to sentiment prediction, and cross-seed analyses show more stable estimates on all three datasets. MoIF connects the organization of multimodal interactions to their statistical reliability through an executable spectral constraint. Code is available at https://github.com/D-ST-Sword/MoIF.

cs.RO [Back]

Abstract: System identification requires expert decisions on the identification method, incorporation of prior physical knowledge, and model validation. We propose ASPIRE, a framework for nonlinear system identification in which a large language model agent selects and executes the identification method. ASPIRE constructs closed-form, nonlinear static or dynamic models from measured data, optionally supplemented by a system description, the intended use of the model, and physical requirements expressed formally or in natural language. Each model includes its parameters, an estimation record, an interpretation of its terms, and an error evaluated on test data inaccessible to the agent. Formal physical requirements are verified independently. ASPIRE operates autonomously or interactively, allowing the agent to ask questions and the user to request further analyses and identification rounds. We evaluate ASPIRE on three measured systems: a mobile robot, a rotational system with dry friction, and a laboratory fermenter. Each system is tested with and without a description, in five runs per task. All 30 runs delivered valid models, at a cost of 0.96 to 3.38 US dollars per run. An assessment by the authors found that, for the 30 models recommended by the agent, one per run, the interpretation of the model and the documentation of its structure selection and parameter estimation fully satisfied 132 of 150 assessed items and partly satisfied 18. The most frequent discrepancy, found in nine models, concerned inaccurate or unsupported claims about model structure selection.

Abstract: Accurate inertial odometry is critical for autonomous systems but suffers from unbounded drift caused by the integration of sensor noise and biases. In approaches such as TLIO, neural pseudo-measurements are trained independently of their downstream filter. This paper introduces Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry (DFIO), a learning framework that embeds the update stages of an error-state Kalman filter directly into a computational graph. Following standalone pre-training, DFIO jointly optimizes the displacement and covariance output heads using posterior-position supervision through differentiable measurement updates. The proposed system is validated on public pedestrian motion datasets featuring both calibrated and uncalibrated inertial measurements. Experimental results on a pedestrian dataset demonstrate that DFIO reduces mean absolute and one-second relative translation errors by 13.2% and 39.6% relative to the TLIO baseline, and by 7.7% and 17.4% relative to its pre-trained counterpart. Furthermore, evaluations on uncalibrated smartphone inertial data indicate that the proposed method effectively mitigates translational drift and maintains trajectory alignment under highly noisy sensor conditions. [116] Elastic-WAM: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Imagination for Efficient Robot Control cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Details

Motivation: 现有 World-Action Model 通过预测未来视觉动态提升机器人控制，但迭代视频去噪带来显著推理开销；已有部分未来去噪方法通常采用固定细化预算，无法适应不同任务、状态和空间区域的重要性差异。

Result: 在多种仿真基准和真实世界长时序操作任务上，Elastic-WAM 取得了 SOTA 控制性能，并降低了推理延迟。

Insight: 创新点在于将世界模型细化计算建模为时空联合的自适应需求场，统一控制停止时机、去噪推进步长和空间刷新区域，并通过三阶段 action-grounded 监督学习该需求场，实现面向动作生成的稀疏世界细化。

Abstract: World-Action Models improve robot control by predicting future visual dynamics, but iterative video denoising incurs substantial inference overhead. Recent approaches reduce this cost through partial future denoising, yet typically rely on fixed refinement budgets. Our empirical observations reveal that preferred refinement depths vary across tasks and states, while different future regions exhibit varying relevance to action generation. Motivated by these findings, we propose Elastic-WAM, which adaptively allocates world refinement computation across temporal denoising time and spatial future space. A unified spatiotemporal refinement demand field governs when to stop world refinement, how far to advance each denoising step, and which regions to refresh. We parameterize this field with a shared temporal basis and spatial amplitudes, and learn it through a three-stage action-grounded supervised procedure. At inference, the learned field guides recurrent sparse world refinement to improve task success while reducing inference overhead. Ilia Larchenko, Gleb Zarin, Atindra Nair, Keerti Manney, Vitaly Kleban

TL;DR: 论文介绍 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge 的参赛方案：在 OmniGibson 仿真中完成 100 个长时程家庭任务。核心是单一 VLA 模型、一个 checkpoint 覆盖全部任务，基于 2025 年第一名的 π0.5 方案改进，并通过更长训练、多任务迁移、方差缩减 flow matching 和云端评测基础设施提升性能；此外将 14 个任务路由给 GPT-6 Astra 编码智能体控制机器人。

Result: 最终排行榜第 9，是前 10 中唯一独立团队。与 2025 共享的 50 个任务上 Q 从 0.260 提升到 0.371，全部 100 个任务 Q 为 0.392。公开提交仅包含部分 Astra 结果，Q 为 0.446，完整任务成功率 27.3%；在路由给 GPT-6 Astra 的 14 个任务上，Astra 平均 Q 为 0.639，而学习策略为 0.108。

Insight: 创新点包括：用单一 VLA checkpoint 覆盖 100 个任务；从 50 到 100 任务迁移时利用技能标注构建语义阶段、用相似旧任务热启动新任务和阶段嵌入，并采用匹配遥操作的 21 维动作空间；提出方差缩减多样本 flow matching，并用简单 noise-vs-compute 模型自适应选择动作专家采样数；构建带溯源、诊断和可恢复状态的云端并行评测基础设施；对部分任务引入编码智能体工具控制。客观看，核心贡献更偏向工程系统与训练规模化，而非全新模型架构。

Abstract: We describe our solution for the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge: 100 long-horizon household tasks in the OmniGibson simulator. We placed 9th on the final leaderboard, the only independent team in the top 10. The core of the solution is a single vision-language-action (VLA) model, one checkpoint for all 100 tasks, built on our 1st-place 2025 solution, which is based on $π_{0.5}$. On the 50 tasks shared with 2025, its Q rises from 0.260 to 0.371; across all 100 tasks it scores 0.392. Our main conclusion from 2025 was that the model was undertrained, so most of the 2026 work went into training the model for longer, optimizing some training parameters, making each training step slightly faster and reducing the gradient noise: a migration from 50 to 100 tasks (semantic stages built from the demonstrations’ skill annotations, task and stage embeddings of new tasks warm-started from similar old ones, and a 21-dimensional action space matching the teleoperation controls); variance-reduced multi-sample flow matching with a simple noise-vs-compute model that picks the number of action-expert samples per observation without ablations; and a 200k-step training run, about one pass over the 211M frames of the demonstrations. A cloud evaluation infrastructure keeps a shared record with provenance, episode diagnostics and restorable simulator states, and runs six simulators on one GPU together faster than real time. On top of the learned policy, 14 tasks are routed to a GPT-6 Astra coding agent that controls the robot through observation and motion tools, starting every episode from a frozen copy of its own practice session on training instances. Across these 14 tasks, Astra’s mean Q is 0.639, against 0.108 for the learned policy. The public submission included only part of the Astra results and scored 0.446 Q with 27.3% full-task success.

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly receive UI screenshots as part of bug reports, yet degraded image quality - caused by low resolution, compression artifacts, or small rendered text - can prevent them from identifying the relevant UI elements and source files. Vision-language models become less reliable as image quality deteriorates, while OCR-assisted approaches may propagate recognition errors from degraded inputs. Both approaches are limited when the visual evidence required for localization is no longer clearly observable in the query screenshot. We propose augmenting coding agents with repository-rendered reference images: crisp screenshots generated from the same codebase that the agent is tasked with modifying. These references provide readable visual evidence of the application’s UI while preserving the current, potentially bug-containing implementation state. Because they are generated from the available repository rather than requiring a known bug-free version, they can be produced automatically for reproducible application states without manual image annotation. We vary screenshot degradation and reference-image provision and measure whether the coding agent identifies the correct source file. The results show that reference images improve file localization especially for degraded image quality, increasing the gold file match rate under fourfold downsampling with JPEG compression. Abstract: Ensembles of ImageNet-pretrained convolutional networks and Vision Transformers are a common way to improve multi-label chest X-ray classification. EVA-X-S alone reached a test macro-AUROC of 0.828; the five-model ensemble reached 0.814 (difference -0.0136, 95% CI -0.0178 to -0.0098) at about 7.8 times the summed backbone latency and ten times the parameters. [120] Counterfactual Analysis of Extent Leakage from Annotation-Derived Prompts in Vision-Language Medical Image Segmentation eess.IV | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Niran Nataraj, Ankita Das, C. Krishna Mohan, Kenji Kawashima

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉-语言医学图像分割中，文本提示可能从标注掩码中泄漏病灶范围信息的问题。作者在结肠镜、乳腺超声和胸部X光上测试两个已发表模型，发现模型主要利用提示中的尺寸、计数、位置或诊断词，尤其依赖与掩码范围相关的信息，并提出可区分提示中图像信息与标签复制信息的评估协议。

Details

Motivation: 公开基准上文本提示能显著提升分割精度，但这些提示中的尺寸、数量、位置等词可能由评估用掩码计算而来，导致标签信息泄漏，夸大了模型对文本理解能力的表现。

Result: 在结肠镜和胸部X光上，仅保留尺寸词或肺区分区短语可恢复相对空提示84%至98%的增益，移除后损失52%至77%；乳腺超声中近半数提示含诊断信息并贡献部分收益。仅用提示词预测病灶面积在7个测试集中的6个达到R² 0.54至0.77；不用测试掩码时，最佳从图像估计范围的方法在结肠镜上达74.59 Dice，低于已发表的77.23。

Insight: 创新点在于用反事实分析量化“范围泄漏”，揭示文本提示可从标注中复制标签范围并影响分割，且该泄漏无需训练模型即可测量；客观来看，提出将提示中真正描述图像的信息与从标签复制来的信息分离的评估协议，对医学多模态分割基准设计有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Text-guided segmentation models receive an image and a short caption about the lesion, and on public benchmarks the caption improves accuracy considerably. On colonoscopy and breast ultrasound, however, the size, count and location words of each caption were computed from the mask that later scores the prediction, and the chest X-ray captions were written by benchmark annotators. We tested what two published models take from them. On colonoscopy and chest X-ray, it is mostly the lesion’s extent. Keeping only the size word or the lung-zone phrase recovers 84 to 98 percent of the improvement over an empty caption, and removing it loses 52 to 77 percent. The rest matters little, and a polyp called a rock is segmented just as well. We call this extent leakage. On breast ultrasound, nearly half the captions also name the diagnosis, which carries part of the benefit. Retraining on three datasets shows that models pick up this habit from their training captions, though only partly on breast ultrasound, and that it weakens their segmentation from the image alone. The leak can be measured without training a model, since caption words alone predict lesion area (coefficient of determination 0.54 to 0.77 on six of seven test sets). Without the test mask, the best method we tested, which estimates extent from the image, reaches 74.59 Dice on colonoscopy against the published 77.23. We propose an evaluation protocol that keeps what a caption says about the image apart from what it copies from the label.

[121] TransGHOST: Real-Time, Synthesis-Free Reconstruction of Instrument-Occluded Regions in Ophthalmic Microsurgery eess.IV | cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Niran Nataraj, Maina Sogabe, Masamichi Yamashita, Norihiko Ito, Kenji Kawashima

TL;DR: 论文提出TransGHOST，一种面向眼科显微手术的因果、参考引导、非生成式遮挡区域重建管线，用于实时恢复器械遮挡下的术中场景。其安全模式通过对仅由可见区域拟合的几何变换重采样已记录帧来填充遮挡像素，并提供逐像素置信度与溯源，对不支持像素进行弃权。方法在新构建的犬白内障手术兽医临床语料上评估，仅对遮挡区域评分，兼顾实时性与可溯源重建。

Details

Motivation: 眼科显微手术中器械进入高倍视野会遮挡组织，影响医生和机器人感知；生成式修补虽能去除遮挡，但会合成缺失内容且难以溯源，许多视频修补还依赖未来帧、无法在线运行。论文旨在实现实时、非合成、可溯源的遮挡区域重建。

Result: 安全模式达到31.89 dB masked PSNR和0.915 masked SSIM，中位延迟51.7 ms、95分位85.5 ms；在预声明留出队列上达到29.83 dB masked PSNR。匹配掩码评估中比LaMa高3.45 dB，区域感知参考选择相比最近参考提升3.60 dB masked PSNR。

Insight: 创新点在于将遮挡恢复建模为基于可见区域几何配准的历史帧重采样问题，避免生成式合成并支持因果在线运行；同时引入逐像素置信度、溯源和弃权机制，以及区域感知参考选择。客观看，其价值在于面向临床可溯源与实时约束的框架设计，但依赖历史参考质量和几何变换假设。

Abstract: Intraoperative scene perception in ophthalmic microsurgery is limited by instrument occlusion: when a tool enters the high-magnification field, it conceals tissue from both the surgeon and the perception components of robot-assisted systems that process the video. Generative inpainting can remove the occluder but replaces the missing image content with synthesis, which is unsuitable when visual provenance matters. Many video inpainters also depend on future frames and cannot operate online. We present TransGHOST, a causal reference-guided reconstruction pipeline whose non-generative safe mode operates in real time. Every restored value is obtained by resampling a recorded frame using geometric transforms fitted only to visible regions. The system provides per-pixel confidence and provenance and marks unsupported pixels as abstentions. We evaluate TransGHOST on a new veterinary clinical corpus of canine cataract surgery with pixel-level instrument and anatomy labels. Only the occluded region is scored, and each scoring target is verified to differ from the reference supplied to the system. Safe mode reaches 31.89 dB masked PSNR and 0.915 masked SSIM at 51.7 ms median and 85.5 ms 95th-percentile latency. On a pre-declared held-out cohort using instrument cutouts excluded from segmentation training, it reaches 29.83 dB masked PSNR, while matched-mask evaluation on validation shows a 3.45 dB advantage over LaMa. Region-aware reference selection improves masked PSNR by 3.60 dB over recency when candidate references can themselves be occluded. Reference-age analysis further defines the operating interval for the brief, repeated occlusions targeted by TransGHOST.
